# Skills · S · part 107

802 entries, sine-profile-dynamics-parameter-optimization to single-cell-rna-seq-clustering. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sine-profile-dynamics-parameter-optimization**: Use when fitting gene dynamics models from scRNA-seq expression data lacking splicing information, by optimizing sine-profile kinetic parameters per target gene via a generalized EM algorithm over TF weights and latent times. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sine-profile-dynamics-parameter-optimization))
- **sinecloud**: First contact with Sine Cloud: choose the right product for the task, prepare least-privilege access, confirm balance and entitlement, then hand off to the product Skill. TRIGGER when the user mentions Sine Cloud / sinecloud / `sinecloud.dev`, or asks to connect an agent to it, create an API key for it, or find out which of Agents, Box, Powers or Gateway does what — without already naming a product task. SKIP when the task already belongs to one product (load that Skill) or is about self-hosting a Sine Cloud fork; this Skill never operates product resources. ([fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills](https://github.com/fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sinecloud))
- **sinecloud-agents**: Run Sine Cloud Agents: pick an available model route, create or reuse an Agent, open a session, submit a turn, follow the live event stream, and read saved items, turns and per-session usage. TRIGGER when the user asks to build/configure an agent on Sine Cloud, run a task through an agent session, choose or change its model, stream or inspect a response, or find out what a session cost. SKIP when the user only needs a one-shot model response (Gateway), a shell command or files without an agent (Box), or a single external tool call (Powers). ([fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills](https://github.com/fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sinecloud-agents))
- **sinecloud-box**: Operate a Sine Cloud Box: list the boxes a key can see, read and write workspace files, submit a command with a caller-generated execution ID, open or extend a Sandbox runtime window, and manage backups. TRIGGER when the user asks to run a command in a cloud box, upload or download its files, start or extend a Sandbox VM window, inspect an execution, or restore a backup. SKIP when the user wants an agent conversation (Agents), a single tool call (Powers), or model output (Gateway); and when no Box exists yet — creating one is a Console action. ([fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills](https://github.com/fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sinecloud-box))
- **sinecloud-gateway**: Call Sine Cloud Gateway: list the models and routes this key can use, send chat, responses, messages, embeddings, image, audio or media requests, and follow asynchronous tasks to a real result. TRIGGER when the user asks to call a model through Sine Cloud, compare or list available models, generate images/audio/video/3D/documents, use an own provider connection, or inspect a Gateway task or budget. SKIP when the user wants an agent session with tools (Agents), a tool call (Powers), or a shell/filespace (Box). ([fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills](https://github.com/fei-bai-inc/sinecloud-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sinecloud-gateway))
- **sinecloud-powers**: Call a Sine Cloud Powers tool: find the real endpoint and its input contract, read the quote, submit one explicit run with a stable identity, and read the saved result. TRIGGER when the user asks to run a tool or integration on Sine Cloud, list what tools exist, inspect a tool's input schema, use their own provider account, or recover an uncertain tool call. SKIP when the user needs model output (Gateway), an agent session (Agents), or a shell/filespace (Box). Trigger on: scRNA-seq, single-cell, cell clustering, cell types, trajectory inference, UMAP, t-SNE, Scanpy, Seurat, doublet detection, cell communication, marker genes, pseudotime, RNA velocity, Leiden clustering, cell atlas, droplet, 10x Genomics, ambient RNA, cell annotation, ligand-receptor, CellChat, highly variable genes, batch correction, neighbor graph. ([mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public](https://github.com/mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public/tree/HEAD/skills/domains/single-cell))
- **single-cell-annotation**: Best practices for single-cell RNA-seq cell type annotation including marker-based, reference-based, and automated classification approaches. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/legacy/single-cell-annotation) · ★394 · 5 copies)
- **single-cell-annotation**: Guide to annotating cell types in single-cell RNA-seq data. Covers manual marker-based, automated (CellTypist, scAnnotate), and reference-based (scArches, Azimuth, SingleR) approaches with decision framework for tool selection. Includes key marker genes for blood/immune, epithelial, and stromal lineages, validation checklist, and common pitfalls. Cross-references: scanpy-scrna-seq for preprocessing, celltypist-cell-annotation for automated classification, scvi-tools-single-cell for deep-learning-based label transfer. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-annotation) · ★1)
- **single-cell-annotation-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when reproducing or extending the active learning benchmark for single-cell cell type annotation, specifically comparing RF vs LR classifiers with random initial 20-cell training selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-annotation-benchmark-reproduction))
- **single-cell-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating or comparing scRNA-seq cell type annotation methods at the cluster level on benchmark datasets (Chen, Xin, Gierahn), or when reproducing the scCATCH vs CellAssign/Garnett/SingleR/scMap/CHETAH consistent-rate table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-annotation-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-annotation-guide**: Decision framework for manual marker-based, automated (CellTypist), and reference-based (popV) cell type annotation in scRNA-seq. Three-tier strategy: Tier 1 manual markers, Tier 2 CellTypist, Tier 3 popV ensemble transfer. Use when planning or troubleshooting annotation. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sciagent/single-cell-annotation-guide) · ★224 · 3 copies)
- **single-cell-annotation-guide**: Cell type annotation strategy for scRNA-seq: name clusters manually from canonical markers, transfer labels from a reference (CellTypist, popV), or do both and validate one against the other. Covers matching tissue and developmental stage, marker evidence, annotation confidence, doublets, unresolved clusters. Use when planning or troubleshooting how to annotate cells. ([FridrichMethod/awesome-skills](https://github.com/FridrichMethod/awesome-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-annotation-guide) · ★17 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-annotation-guide**: Decision framework for choosing between manual marker-based, automated (CellTypist), and reference-based (popV) cell type annotation strategies in single-cell RNA-seq analysis. Covers a three-tier strategy: Tier 1 (manual canonical markers), Tier 2 (CellTypist pre-trained models), and Tier 3 (popV ensemble label transfer). Use when planning or troubleshooting cell type annotation in any scRNA-seq project. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-annotation-guide) · ★1)
- **single-cell-annotation-metadata-parsing**: Use when a single-cell gene expression matrix must be paired with a cell-label metafile so that expression can be aggregated per cell type prior to cell-to-cell communication analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-annotation-metadata-parsing))
- **single-cell-annotation-scoring**: Use when you have reference data with cell-type labels ('cell_type' column in AnnData) and need to annotate a query single-cell dataset by scoring cells against ranked marker genes rather than training a model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-annotation-scoring))
- **single-cell-annotation-skills-with-omicverse**: Guide Claude through SCSA, MetaTiME, CellVote, CellMatch, GPTAnno, and weighted KNN transfer workflows for annotating single-cell modalities. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-annotation) · ★3.1k · 19 copies)
- **single-cell-annotation-stabilization**: Use when a Seurat scRNA-seq object has root-node cell-type predictions from a Census pretrained model and noisy per-cell labels need to be stabilized using UMAP clusters and prediction contours before continuing hierarchical annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-annotation-stabilization))
- **single-cell-annotation-tool-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how accurately established cell-annotation tools (e.g. ACTINN, SingleR, SCSA) classify tumor T/NK or myeloid scRNA-seq cell subtypes relative to scWizard on independent datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-annotation-tool-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-annotation-transfer**: Use when a newly generated scRNA-seq query dataset must be labeled by transferring annotations from a curated reference AnnData, and interpretable per-cell attention (e.g., TF-regulon tokens for trajectory analysis) is desired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-annotation-transfer))
- **single-cell-apd-dynamics-interpretation**: Use when a PACdataset of single-cell poly(A) sites (e.g. from Sierra or scAPAtrap) exists and the goal is to interpret APA dynamics — 3'UTR shortening, differential pA usage, or APA markers — across cell types or biological stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-apd-dynamics-interpretation))
- **single-cell-as-probability-matrix-imputation**: Use when a single-cell AS probability matrix (rows = AS sites, columns = cells) produced from junction reads contains NA entries from dropouts and an imputed, NA-indicator-annotated matrix is needed before unsupervised clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-as-probability-matrix-imputation))
- **single-cell-atac-cell-type-signature-enrichment**: Use when a selected cisTopic LDA model exists for a scATAC-seq dataset and you must quantify per-cell enrichment of external epigenomic signatures (marker gene sets or bulk ATAC profiles) to assign or visualize cell-type identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-cell-type-signature-enrichment))
- **single-cell-atac-copy-number-calling**: Use when you have a normalized binned single-cell ATAC-seq matrix and need to call CNVs per cell to identify malignant subclones, focal amplifications, or arm-level chromosome gains/losses without a matched control file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-copy-number-calling))
- **single-cell-atac-data-processing**: Use when raw scATAC-seq fragment files need conversion into filtered, normalized, binned accessibility matrices as input to CNV calling or subclonal deconvolution with Copy-scAT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-data-processing))
- **single-cell-atac-enhancer-classification**: Use when single-cell ATAC peak sets need to be separated into cell-type-specific CREs and you need an interpretable, motif-based model that names the TF motifs (e.g. MEF2C, NKX2-5, GATA, FLI1) driving the classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-enhancer-classification))
- **single-cell-atac-fragment-import-processing**: Use when you have aligned single-cell ATAC-seq data as BAM files or fragment files (TSV format with genomic coordinates) and need to prepare it for spectral embedding, clustering, and peak calling. This is the entry point after alignment but before any dimension reduction or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/single-cell-atac-fragment-import-processing))
- **single-cell-atac-gene-score-modeling**: Use when you have a target scATAC-seq gene score feature matrix plus first-round Cellcano predictions (probabilities and labels) and must retrain a teacher-student KD model on low-entropy anchor cells to label all non-anchor cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-gene-score-modeling))
- **single-cell-atac-lsi-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a single-cell chromatin accessibility (scATAC) count matrix from a combinatorial-indexing assay (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-atac-lsi-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-atac-peak-frequency-filtering**: Use when a raw scATAC-seq peak-by-cell count matrix (cells × peaks, AnnData with peaks as n_vars) must be normalized and denoised before feeding a variational autoencoder or cross-modality translation model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-peak-frequency-filtering))
- **single-cell-atac-seq-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when after loading and preprocessing raw scATAC-seq data into an ArchR project object when you need to compute low-dimensional embeddings for clustering, UMAP/tSNE visualization, or integrated multi-omic analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/single-cell-atac-seq-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-atac-seq-imputation**: Use when the input is a sparse, near-binary scATAC-seq peak-by-cell count matrix (h5ad, txt/tsv/csv, or mtx folder) and the goal is denoising/imputation of missing accessibility values for downstream correlation or clustering evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-seq-imputation))
- **single-cell-atac-seq-peak-calling-annotaion**: Call accessible chromatin peaks from ATAC-seq BAM files, annotate peaks to genomic features and genes, and identify differentially accessible regions between experimental conditions. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-atac-seq-peak-calling-annotaion) · ★1.1k)
- **single-cell-atac-seq-qc-processing**: Trim adapters, align reads, remove duplicates and mitochondrial contamination, and evaluate chromatin accessibility data quality before calling peaks. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-atac-seq-qc-processing) · ★1.1k)
- **single-cell-atac-tf-activity-estimation**: Use when a single-cell ATAC-seq peak matrix (e.g. from a paired multiome Seurat object such as coembed.trajectory.rds) must be converted into per-cell TF activity scores to prioritize candidate TFs for enhancer-based GRN inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-tf-activity-estimation))
- **single-cell-atac-topic-model-interpretation**: Use when a fitted cisTopic object (e.g. cisTopicObject_pbmc.Rds with a selected 30-topic model) needs biological interpretation: assigning functions, master regulators, or cell-type signatures to each topic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-atac-topic-model-interpretation))
- **single-cell-atlas**: Full single-cell RNA-seq pipeline from raw counts to biological interpretation. Covers QC, normalization, batch integration, clustering, annotation, pseudobulk DE, trajectory inference, cell-cell communication, and TF activity. Dual-language: Seurat v5 (R) and scanpy (Python). ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/zamushwani/single-cell-atlas) · ★224 · 3 copies)
- **single-cell-autoencoder-latent-space-training**: Use when you need to functionally mine scRNAseq clusters with a knowledge-biased autoencoder: you have a filtered, log10-transformed counts table plus reference cluster assignments and want per-cluster reconstruction quality (QCC/QCM) and TF signatures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-autoencoder-latent-space-training))
- **single-cell-bam-quality-filtering**: Use when processing split single-cell BAMs from a sci-L3-Strand-seq run and you must decide which sorted cells pass strand-state QC before downstream SCE counting, phasing, or coverage aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-bam-quality-filtering))
- **single-cell-barcode-deconvolution**: Use when starting a single-cell long-read analysis: you have packaged FASTQ files from PacBio or ONT runs plus a barcode-to-celltype assignment file (e.g. from Seurat on the paired 10x v2 short-read data), and need barcoded, cell-type-labeled reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-deconvolution))
- **single-cell-barcode-downsampling**: Use when you need to determine the minimum number of cells from a deconvoluted single-cell cluster that still supports reliable genetic identification, e.g. testing identification sensitivity on 10X barcodes_reduced.txt files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-downsampling))
- **single-cell-barcode-extraction**: Use when raw paired-end single-cell amplicon FASTQ from an expressed synthetic barcoding experiment must be converted into per-cell lineage barcode annotation and clone-size tables, e.g. to cross-validate an existing pipeline like FateMap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-extraction))
- **single-cell-barcode-oligo-assignment**: Use when input is hashed single-cell CITE-seq ADT FASTQ data (I1/R1/R2) and per-cell hashtag oligo UMI counts are needed as input to hashing demultiplexing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-oligo-assignment))
- **single-cell-barcode-pcr-chimera-filtering**: Use when processing single-cell lineage-barcode reads where PCR chimeras inflate barcodes-per-cell and UMI sequencing errors inflate UMI counts, and an accurate per-cell barcode annotation table is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-pcr-chimera-filtering))
- **single-cell-barcode-qc-visualization**: Use when processing single-cell (10x-style) FASTQs containing expressed synthetic lineage barcodes and you need chimeras removed, UMIs error-corrected, cells annotated with barcodes, and QC plots of barcodes per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-qc-visualization))
- **single-cell-barcode-quality-control**: Use when starting from a raw, unfiltered droplet-based scRNA-seq count matrix (mtx or h5, e.g. neurons_900_raw.h5) and you need to define the P/U barcode sets that EmptyNN requires before training its classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-quality-control))
- **single-cell-barcode-quality-filtering**: Use when raw cellranger-atac fragment files must be converted into a filtered, binned coverage matrix and low-quality cell barcodes need removal prior to single-cell CNV inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-quality-filtering))
- **single-cell-barcode-search-configuration**: Use when processing concatenated long-read single-cell cDNA data (PacBio MAS-Seq/MAS-ISO-seq or ONT) with nanoranger and a protocol-specific adapter barcode pattern (e.g., TruSeq adapter) must be configured to segment reads and recover cell barcodes/UMIs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-barcode-search-configuration))
- **single-cell-barcode-table-processing**: Use when you have barcoded single-cell profiles with a table of unique cell IDs and assigned barcodes, and you want clonal relationships to inform clustering (alpha) or UMAP embeddings (Warp Factor s in 0-10). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-table-processing))
- **single-cell-barcode-umi-annotation**: Use when processing long-read FASTQ from 10x Genomics 5'-anchored whole-transcriptome cDNA libraries or targeted amplicons and you need per-cell, per-UMI assignment of reads before calling SNVs, fusions, isoforms, or CAR/TCR sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-barcode-umi-annotation))
- **single-cell-barcode-umi-position-indexing**: Use when FASTQ reads must be routed into a single-cell preprocessing tool (STARsolo, kb/kallisto bustools) that needs explicit barcode/UMI/cDNA start,end,strand positions, and an assay spec.yaml exists or can be authored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-barcode-umi-position-indexing))
- **single-cell-batch-correction-benchmarking**: Use when comparing how well batch-effect correction methods mix datasets with overlapping cell types while preserving cell-type resolution, e.g. reproducing Cell BLAST Fig. 2a-style integration benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-correction-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-batch-correction-fastmnn**: Use when single-cell histone-modification datasets (e.g. scCUT&Tag GSE124680 vs GSE124690 replicates) contain distinct technical batches that must be merged before UMAP projection or clustering without losing true K562-vs-H1 biological separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-correction-fastmnn))
- **single-cell-batch-effect-correction**: Use when raw read-count matrices from multiple scRNA-seq batches show batch separation (e.g., in t-SNE colored by batch) and the goal is corrected counts plus cell-type clustering without a priori subtype labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-effect-correction--4b2f794ba9ec9aa5))
- **single-cell-batch-effect-correction**: Use when scRNA-seq data from multiple scRNA-seq protocols or batches must be integrated for clustering, and batch mixing must be quantified (e.g., ASW) against baselines such as DESC, Harmony, MNN, scVI or Scanorama. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-effect-correction--62e7d7e3afc5b3f9))
- **single-cell-batch-effect-embedding-training**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data from multiple batches requires a nonlinear lower-dimensional embedding that explicitly models batch effects for downstream batch-effect assessment metrics like iLISI or kBET. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-effect-embedding-training))
- **single-cell-batch-effect-integration**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices from multiple batches (e.g., pancreas, human immune, lung, heart atlas) must be integrated to remove batch effects while conserving cell types, trajectories, or rare populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-effect-integration))
- **single-cell-batch-effect-quantification**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data span multiple batches (e.g. 10X 3pV1/3pV2/5p, five islet platforms inDrop/Fluidigm C1/Smart-Seq2/CEL-seq/CEL-seq2) and you need rejection-rate and mixing metrics in both PCA and nonlinear embedding space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-effect-quantification))
- **single-cell-batch-integration**: Integrate multiple scRNA-seq samples/batches using Harmony, scVI, Seurat anchors, and fastMNN. Remove technical variation while preserving biological differences. Use when integrating multiple scRNA-seq batches or datasets. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/batch-integration) · ★1)
- **single-cell-batch-integration**: Use when two scRNA-seq count matrices from different technologies (e.g., CEL-Seq2 and Smart-Seq2) need batch correction before joint analysis, and cell-type mixing (iLISI), cell-type consistency (cLISI), and local-structure retention (k-RNN) must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-integration--81ab09ee9fa3636a))
- **single-cell-batch-integration**: Use when a paired control/perturbed scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. GSE164240 PMC cells treated with MK-886, or simulated control + simulate.perturb cells) must be clustered so treatment-affected states are resolved across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-integration--c4f3ca355d18902f))
- **single-cell-batch-integration-benchmarking**: Use when quantitatively comparing data-integration methods (Scanorama, scVI, Seurat) against a trajectory-tree-alignment method on paired single-cell datasets, measuring biological variance conservation with the ATC score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-integration-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-batch-integration-joint-clustering**: Use when multi-donor or multi-batch scRNA/snRNA-seq datasets must be integrated for joint clustering, and interpretable gene factors/modules (not just a latent embedding) are needed for cell-type annotation or marker comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-integration-joint-clustering))
- **single-cell-batch-integration-topic-modeling**: Use when a perturbation single-cell atlas spans multiple sequencing batches with cell states batch-confounded (e.g., wild-type vs Tal1-/- chimeric mouse embryo batches) and PCA-based latent spaces fail to mix batches while preserving cell-type separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-integration-topic-modeling))
- **single-cell-batch-mixing-metric-implementation**: Use when evaluating batch mixing of integrated single-cell RNA-seq datasets with imbalanced cell type proportions, where standard cell type-agnostic iLISI scores are mis and a per-cell-type normalized metric is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-mixing-metric-implementation))
- **single-cell-batch-subsampling**: Use when you need to test whether an integration method's clustering or batch-mixing metrics depend on how many donor batches (or which subsets) are provided as input, e.g., for consensus-PCA-based methods where each sample's initial decomposition depends on the other samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-batch-subsampling))
- **single-cell-benchmark-dataset-handling**: Use when a new or adapted scRNAseq method needs objective evaluation against known cell identities: benchmark datasets with published ground-truth labels and ARI-based cluster recovery scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-benchmark-dataset-handling))
- **single-cell-benchmark-dataset-loading**: Use when a study needs the Goolam or Yan scRNA-seq matrices (or the MPSSC benchmark .mat sets) with cell/true_labs labels to benchmark similarity learning and clustering methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-benchmark-dataset-loading))
- **single-cell-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when inferred trajectories (e.g. MST-based trajectories from clustered single-cell data) must be scored against known ground-truth topologies from the dynverse Zenodo benchmark deposit (10.5281/zenodo.1443566). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-benchmark-evaluation--a98c4cbb7ec1eba8))
- **single-cell-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell-type classification methods on labeled scRNA-seq datasets: you need a repeated 7:3 train/test benchmark with per-dataset accuracy/balanced-accuracy tables aggregating 50 repeats. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-benchmark-evaluation--bbae3ead8130b7b9))
- **single-cell-benchmark-result-reproduction**: Use when a benchmark table of per-dataset annotation accuracies is available with dataset metadata (cell counts, cell type counts, label distributions) and a reported correlation (e.g., accuracy vs. DKL) must be independently reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-benchmark-result-reproduction))
- **single-cell-benchmark-score-normalization**: Use when you have a methods x metrics x datasets matrix of raw evaluation scores from simulating scRNA-seq/SRT data (8 metrics over 15 data properties) and must turn it into comparable per-method accuracy scores in [0,1]. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-benchmark-score-normalization))
- **single-cell-benchmarking-f1-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare a single-cell (scRNA-seq, snRNA-seq, or snATAC-seq gene activity) cell type classifier against established baselines using held-out validation F1 on annotated, pre-split benchmark datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-benchmarking-f1-evaluation))
- **single-cell-best-practices**: The complete "Single-cell best practices" book (sc-best-practices.org, Theis lab) as reference files, with each chapter's bibliography. Use when analyzing or advising on single-cell or spatial omics data - scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq, CITE-seq, immune repertoire (TCR/BCR), spatial transcriptomics, multimodal - including raw data processing, QC, doublets, ambient RNA, normalization, feature selection, dimensionality reduction, clustering, cell type annotation, data integration and batch correction, pseudotime, RNA velocity, lineage tracing, differential expression (pseudobulk), compositional analysi… ([lutrarutra/codeflower-sandbox](https://github.com/lutrarutra/codeflower-sandbox/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-best-practices))
- **single-cell-biologist**: Think and work like an expert Single-Cell Biologist. Use when a task calls for Single-Cell Biologist judgment. Reasons from assay chemistry, sample-level replication, cell-state manifolds, and metadata provenance; treats ambient RNA, doublets, dissociation stress, batch, and pseudoreplication as core failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/single-cell-biologist/skills/single-cell-biologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-biologist**: Expert-thinking profile for Single-Cell Biologist (wet-lab / computational single-cell genomics): Reasons from assay chemistry, sample-level replication, cell-state manifolds, and metadata provenance; treats ambient RNA, doublets, dissociation stress, batch, and pseudoreplication as core failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-biologist) · ★13)
- **single-cell-biologist**: Reasons from assay chemistry, sample-level replication, cell-state manifolds, and metadata provenance; treats ambient RNA, doublets, dissociation stress, batch, and pseudoreplication as core failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-biologist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **single-cell-biologist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for single cell biologist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/single-cell-biologist))
- **single-cell-bisulfite-methylation-quantification**: Use when single-cell bisulfite methylation matrices must be coupled to an independently recovered single-cell ordering (e.g., reCAT pseudo time-series from paired scRNA-seq) to visualize methylation dynamics along a cyclic biological process. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-bisulfite-methylation-quantification))
- **single-cell-boolean-network-rule-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq data (log2(TPM+1)-style matrix with cell IDs and condition labels) must be converted into executable Boolean models of KEGG (or custom GRAPHML) signaling pathways to prioritize high-impact genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-boolean-network-rule-inference))
- **single-cell-boxplot-significance-annotation**: Use when you have cell-level group assignments (e.g., DICE-derived primed vs unprimed TACs) plus continuous per-cell scores such as Palantir fate probabilities or chromVAR TF activity, and need boxplots with annotated one-sided Wilcoxon significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-boxplot-significance-annotation))
- **single-cell-branch-probability-extraction**: Use when a VIA trajectory has been run on single-cell omics data and you need per-cell fate assignments or branch probabilities toward detected terminal states to score lineage/fate resolution against annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-branch-probability-extraction))
- **single-cell-bulk-gep-correlation-matching**: Use when you have scRNA-seq-derived tumor sub-cluster GEPs plus LINCS L1000 bulk perturbation profiles and need to identify drug combinations killing the maximum number of tumor sub-clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-bulk-gep-correlation-matching))
- **single-cell-cancer-signature-module-scoring**: Use when cells have already received a cancer-type annotation from scATOMIC's terminal layer and you must separate malignant cells from normal cells sharing the same tissue of origin in a tumour biopsy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cancer-signature-module-scoring))
- **single-cell-cdna-library-pipeline-execution**: Use when input FASTQ/BAM reads come from 10x Genomics whole-transcriptome cDNA libraries or targeted amplicons sequenced on ONT or Pacific Biosciences devices and need nanoranger processing, including multimer deconcatenation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-cdna-library-pipeline-execution))
- **single-cell-cell-annotation**: Automated cell type annotation using reference-based methods including CellTypist, scPred, SingleR, and Azimuth for consistent, reproducible cell labeling. Use when automatically annotating cell types using reference datasets. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/cell-annotation) · ★1)
- **single-cell-cell-communication**: Infer cell-cell communication networks from scRNA-seq data using CellChat, NicheNet, and LIANA for ligand-receptor interaction analysis. Use when inferring ligand-receptor interactions between cell types. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/cell-communication) · ★1)
- **single-cell-cell-communication-inference**: Use when the input is time-course or pseudotime-ordered single-cell RNA-seq without definitive cell types (e.g. pre-blastocyst embryos) and the goal is to infer causal ligand-receptor communication and its link to temporal TF programs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-communication-inference))
- **single-cell-cell-count-simulation**: Use when you need to determine how many pseudo-replicates Scanpro's bootstrapping should use for an unreplicated single-cell dataset, given an estimate of total cells per condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-count-simulation))
- **single-cell-cell-cycle-phase-scoring**: Use when you have a single-cell cell×gene matrix (e.g., MAPIT interaction scores or expression from scMAPIT-seq, such as 1,499 cells) plus matched transcriptome data and need to stratify cells by cell-cycle phase and find phase-enriched clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-cell-cycle-phase-scoring))
- **single-cell-cell-cycle-scoring**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset (e.g., the 145-cell Llorens-Bobadilla neural stem cell data) contains quiescent G0-like cells that standard cell-cycle analysis does not detect and must be removed before pseudotime inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-cycle-scoring))
- **single-cell-cell-type-annotation**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (Seurat object) needs fully automated cell-type labels, or when cancer datasets need malignant cell identification and cell-of-origin inference, without manual marker curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-type-annotation--55d50b14fe1a00fa))
- **single-cell-cell-type-annotation**: Use when a pre-processed, clustered Seurat object from plant single-cell data needs cell-type labels without manual curation, especially when a reference atlas (e.g., PRJNA517021, PRJNA706435) or a marker gene data frame is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-type-annotation--be0df0ab83a76c39))
- **single-cell-cell-type-annotation-transfer**: Use when raw source scRNA-seq data are unavailable (privacy/storage) but pre-trained source model weights exist, and target data may contain novel cell types requiring source-free annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-type-annotation-transfer))
- **single-cell-cell-type-classification**: Use when you have raw (unnormalized) single-cell count data (Seurat .rds, scanpy .h5ad, or 10x cellranger output) and annotated cell type labels to train on, or a pretrained Pollock module to assign cell type labels to new cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-type-classification))
- **single-cell-cell-type-interpretability-scoring**: Use when you have a trained Pollock VAE classification module (e.g. scRNAseq_brca_panimmune) plus raw-count .h5ad/.RDS cells and need the interpretability scores identifying which genes drive each predicted cell state. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-type-interpretability-scoring))
- **single-cell-cell-type-label-transfer**: Use when a query AnnData needs cell type labels transferred from an annotated reference (e.g. resolving monocyte subtypes in pan-cancer tumor-infiltrating myeloid data), and you want interpretable, pathway-token-based predictions with per-cell probabilities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cell-type-label-transfer))
- **single-cell-cellphonedb-communication-mapping**: Run omicverse's CellPhoneDB v5 wrapper on annotated single-cell data to infer ligand-receptor networks and produce CellChat-style visualisations. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cellphone-db) · ★3.1k · 18 copies)
- **single-cell-cellstate-label-transfer**: Use when raw scRNA-seq counts of T cells (or other cell types with a pre-built reference map) must be annotated with reference-defined cell states, e.g. assigning tumor-specific CD8 TILs to Tex/Tpex/EM states on the murine TIL atlas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cellstate-label-transfer))
- **single-cell-celltype-annotation-by-correlation**: Use when you have a 10x 3' gene-cell-barcode count matrix (e.g. from Cell Ranger) and need per-cell type labels by matching expression profiles to reference populations, or to stratify cell types by host/donor genotype. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-celltype-annotation-by-correlation))
- **single-cell-celltype-group-comparison**: Use when pseudobulk per-cell-type isoform counts from long-read single-cell data are available for two cell groups and the goal is to find genes with differential transcript usage (gDTU) and per-transcript significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-celltype-group-comparison))
- **single-cell-celltyping-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when validating or reproducing reported accuracy comparisons of scATAC-seq celltyping methods (e.g., Cellcano vs scJoint vs ACTINN) from a figure's Source Data file, specifically for tasks whose target is a FACS-sorted gold-standard dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-celltyping-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-chromatin-data-handling**: Use when after calling peaks and annotating cells in an ArchR project, when you need to perform trajectory analysis using STREAM or other external tools that require a peak-by-cell matrix in a specific tabular format (CSV or TSV) rather than native ArchR objects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/single-cell-chromatin-data-handling))
- **single-cell-chromatin-sample-filtering**: Use when after loading fragment counts into a SummarizedExperiment object (e.g., via getCounts) but before motif matching or deviation computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/single-cell-chromatin-sample-filtering))
- **single-cell-classifier-training-caret**: Use when a single-cell (RNA or CyTOF) expression matrix needs a probabilistic classifier for active learning uncertainty sampling, pseudo-labelling, or entropy-based mislabel detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-classifier-training-caret))
- **single-cell-clonal-genotyping-from-transcriptomes**: Use when single-cell gene expression data carry linked per-cell mutation calls (GoT) that are noisy (false negatives from shallow sequencing, false positives from ambient RNA) and the goal is to reconcile genotype-based clone labels with transcriptional cell-type annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clonal-genotyping-from-transcriptomes))
- **single-cell-clonal-subset-comparison**: Use when single cells have already been assigned to clones/subclones (e.g., mtDNA subclones clone1.MT0 vs clone1.MT1) and the goal is to test whether those subclones are biologically distinct via DE genes and Hallmark pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clonal-subset-comparison))
- **single-cell-clone-calling-from-lineage-barcode-data**: Use when single-cell scRNA-seq data carry integrated lineage barcodes (e.g. CellTag lentiviral captures from 10x Chromium runs) and you must group cells into clones for state-fate or clonal GRN analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clone-calling-from-lineage-barcode-data))
- **single-cell-cluster-annotation**: Use when a preprocessed and clustered Seurat object or count matrix from scRNA-seq or imaging-based spatial data (e.g., MERFISH mouse brain) needs cell type labels per cluster or per cell, and marker-gene-based annotation with interpretable results is desired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-annotation--a8e3812288b675af))
- **single-cell-cluster-annotation**: Use when a Seurat (or equivalent) single-cell object has been clustered (e.g. 11 BALF clusters from GSE145926) and cluster labels must be assigned to cell types before stratified DE testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-annotation--d780589620167a4d))
- **single-cell-cluster-annotation-propagation**: Use when a clustered scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., a Seurat object like demo_sc_data) lacks cell-type labels and cell-cell communication analysis with scCrossTalk requires a per-cell sc_celltype character vector. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-annotation-propagation))
- **single-cell-cluster-assignment-counting**: Use when evaluating sequence-search or demultiplexing tools on single-cell FASTQ data by measuring how many cells in a known cluster are recovered via their barcodes after matching target sequences such as BCAS4-BCAS3, E1A, or a variant allele. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-assignment-counting))
- **single-cell-cluster-biomarker-ranking**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset has been clustered into cell subtypes (e.g. CD163-high macrophage clusters identified by scNovel) and you need pathway-level (GO/GSEA) interpretation of condition-dependent differential expression per cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-biomarker-ranking))
- **single-cell-cluster-connectivity-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset or 2D embedding must be screened before trajectory fitting — e.g., before Slingshot/Monocle3 — to flag disconnected clusters or homogeneous cells with no trajectory signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-connectivity-analysis))
- **single-cell-cluster-count-aggregation**: Use when starting PILOT-style sample-level analysis: you have a cell-by-cluster annotated .h5ad (e.g. clusters_col 'cell_subtype', sample_col 'sampleID') and need per-sample cluster probability distributions for Wasserstein distance computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-count-aggregation))
- **single-cell-cluster-count-validation**: Use when a published scRNA-seq result specifies an exact cell count and cluster number for a defined cell compartment (e.g., 44,799 T/NK cells in 32 clusters) and the goal is to independently reproduce and check those values in Seurat. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-count-validation))
- **single-cell-cluster-count-verification**: Use when scRNA-seq data has been deliberately over-clustered with Leiden at high resolution (e.g. 3.5) and you need to verify how many significantly different clusters remain after Cytocipher merging and whether intermediate cell states were recovered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-count-verification))
- **single-cell-cluster-functional-annotation**: Use when a clustered scRNAseq counts matrix needs functional annotation — e.g. identifying which TFs, miRNAs, kinases, or immune signatures drive cell clusterization, or extracting minimal COMET marker gene panels (≤4 genes) for specific clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-functional-annotation))
- **single-cell-cluster-gene-ranking**: Use when you have an AnnData object with predicted cell-cluster labels from a scRNA-seq clustering method and need per-cluster marker genes to annotate cell types against a curated marker database like CellMark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-gene-ranking))
- **single-cell-cluster-homogeneity-quantification**: Use when a scDEED-optimized t-SNE/UMAP visualization raises the question of whether a visual cluster is trustworthy or should be divided, and you need a numeric purity score per cluster and per merged cluster combination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-homogeneity-quantification))
- **single-cell-cluster-interpretation**: Use when a learned SIMLR similarity matrix (or single-cell scRNA-seq expression matrix with high noise/dropout) must be converted into 2-D/3-D low-dimensional coordinates and scatter plots that reveal subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-interpretation))
- **single-cell-cluster-label-aggregation**: Use when you have a latent cell embedding (e.g. from a dual denoising autoencoder) and K basic clusterings with varying k, and need a single consensus partition that is stable across random seeds and cluster counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-label-aggregation))
- **single-cell-cluster-label-mapping**: Use when synthetic single-cell observations must be assigned cluster identities for fidelity evaluation, and ground-truth cluster labels (e.g. from Seurat clustering on UMAP of PBMC4k or Segerstolpe data) exist only for the original cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-label-mapping))
- **single-cell-cluster-label-validation**: Use when a scRNA-seq clustering method (e.g., SSRE) has produced cell type labels and you need to confirm the labels are biologically meaningful by recovering known marker genes via differential expression testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-label-validation))
- **single-cell-cluster-level-statistics**: Use when CITE-seq data with normalized RNA and ADT matrices plus per-cell cluster assignments needs a quantitative per-cluster purity assessment in both transcriptome and surface protein modalities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-level-statistics))
- **single-cell-cluster-marker-gene-identification**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq expression matrix with predefined cluster labels and need a reliable per-cluster marker gene set before cell type annotation, especially to avoid pseudo marker genes from one-against-all comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-marker-gene-identification))
- **single-cell-cluster-marker-identification**: Use when a Seurat/scMiko scRNA-seq object has author- or algorithm-defined cluster labels (e.g. 'sub_cluster') and you need cluster-specific DEGs, or want to benchmark CDI (binary DEG) markers against Wilcoxon markers for specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-marker-identification--5aec6f64bdb78e74))
- **single-cell-cluster-marker-identification**: Use when a clustered scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., Seurat object with FindClusters-defined clusters) needs cluster-level marker genes to feed downstream annotation tools such as deCS that accept either gene lists or z-scored expression profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-marker-identification--b41fde1183680047))
- **single-cell-cluster-marker-roc-analysis**: Use when you have a count matrix plus cluster assignments (e.g., ClonoCluster hybrid clusters across alpha values) and need to quantify marker sensitivity/specificity via AUC or identify reorganization markers (reorg-AUC &gt; 0.80). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-marker-roc-analysis))
- **single-cell-cluster-pooling-and-preparation**: Use when scRNA-seq data stored as a Seurat object must be converted into pooled, cluster-level transcriptomic profiles (rows = genes, columns = clusters) before predicting miRNA expression with miRSCAPE or a similar trained model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-pooling-and-preparation))
- **single-cell-cluster-reconstruction-quality-metric-evaluation**: Use when an scRNAseq or spatial transcriptomics counts table has been clustered (e.g., 9 SIMLR clusters on a Visium breast cancer section) and you need to determine which clusters the SCA autoencoder reconstructs for the majority of cells before downstream functional annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-reconstruction-quality-metric-evaluation))
- **single-cell-cluster-specific-network-inference**: Use when you have a scATAC-seq-derived base GRN edge list plus a scRNA-seq expression matrix (e.g. Mouse sci-ATAC-seq Atlas with the Biddy et al. 2018 MEF-to-iEP data) and need per-cluster regulatory networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-specific-network-inference))
- **single-cell-cluster-stability-integration**: Use when an AnnData object carries ≥2 conflicting cell-cluster annotation columns (e.g. Leiden at different resolutions, Seurat3/Monocle3/ICGS2, cellHarmony label transfers, or RNA/ADT modality labels) and a single high-confidence reconciled assignment is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-stability-integration))
- **single-cell-cluster-visualization**: Use when single-cell profiles (e.g. CSV expression matrix plus discrete class labels) must be visualized in 2D while preserving both cluster structure and differentiation pathways, especially when t-SNE/PCA/MDS fail to reveal trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cluster-visualization))
- **single-cell-clustering**: Dimensionality reduction and clustering for single-cell RNA-seq using Seurat (R) and Scanpy (Python). Use for running PCA, computing neighbors, clustering with Leiden/Louvain algorithms, generating UMAP/tSNE embeddings, and visualizing clusters. Use when performing dimensionality reduction and clustering on single-cell data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/clustering) · ★1)
- **single-cell-clustering-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when you need to judge whether a single-cell clustering/joint-embedding method (e.g. CoupledNMF, Cell Ranger, Seurat V3) produces subpopulations consistent with external labels derived from FACS-sorted bulk signatures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-accuracy-evaluation))
- **single-cell-clustering-across-multiple-individuals**: Use when clustering raw UMI count matrices from droplet-based scRNA-seq (e.g., 10x Genomics Chromium) generated for several individuals or donors, and naive pooling would cause batch effects or Cell Ranger aggregation downsampling would lose information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-across-multiple-individuals))
- **single-cell-clustering-across-treatments**: Use when a single-cell perturbation dataset contains control cells plus one or more treatments and you need control-anchored, interpretable cell states — especially when standard integration workflows might erase perturbation-unique responses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-across-treatments))
- **single-cell-clustering-algorithm-benchmarking**: Use when you have precomputed cell embeddings (e.g. SnapCCESS snapshots across ensemble sizes) for multimodal single-cell data and need to compare clustering algorithms against known cell-type labels using ARI and NMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-algorithm-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-clustering-algorithm-comparison**: Use when comparing clustering method performance on single-cell (mass cytometry or scRNA-seq) datasets with known cell-type labels, or when testing whether a clustering method stays accurate at large scale via subsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-algorithm-comparison))
- **single-cell-clustering-analysis**: Use when scATAC clustering may be driven by CNVs rather than genuine accessibility states, and you need to test cluster stability by removing CNV-bearing chromosome peaks and comparing pre/post-reclustering assignments via AMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-analysis))
- **single-cell-clustering-and-batch-correction-with-omicverse**: Guide Claude through omicverse's single-cell clustering workflow, covering preprocessing, QC, multimethod clustering, topic modeling, cNMF, and cross-batch integration as demonstrated in t_cluster.ipynb and t_single_batch.ipynb. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-clustering) · ★3.1k · 17 copies)
- **single-cell-clustering-and-dam-annotation**: Use when a combined gene + TE single-cell expression matrix needs clustering, UMAP visualization, and cell-cluster annotation, e.g. identifying DAM clusters in the APP/PS1 AD mouse model via Cst7, Lpl, and Clec7a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-and-dam-annotation))
- **single-cell-clustering-annotation-workflow**: Use when a raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrix must be quality-filtered, normalized, reduced to a PCA embedding, clustered by a neighborhood graph, tested for cluster-specific markers, and annotated against an external tissue marker reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/workflows/single-cell-clustering-annotation))
- **single-cell-clustering-based-subsampling**: Use when a single-cell expression dataset is highly imbalanced and a small, balanced subsample of cells must be chosen for manual labeling, with or without prior marker gene knowledge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-based-subsampling))
- **single-cell-clustering-benchmark-against-known-cell-types**: Use when a clustering (or cell-state) assignment on scRNA-seq data must be validated against an external reference cell-type labeling, e.g. marker-derived labels for PBMCs, using ARI/NMI/purity metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-benchmark-against-known-cell-types))
- **single-cell-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when clustering scATAC-seq data (e.g. the Forebrain.h5ad dataset, 2088 cells, k=8) and you need to compare SCALE against scABC, scVI, cisTopic, or SC3 using ARI/NMI/F1 metrics against reference labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when clustering single-cell RNA-seq/multiome data (~10,000-cell PBMC or large-scale MCA) and you need to compare cluster quality (ARI, purity) against baselines or quantify label-confidence filtering gains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-benchmarking--d4314ce74fc8a22e))
- **single-cell-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when comparing clustering performance of multiple single-cell (multimodal or single-modal) methods on datasets with known ground-truth cell-type labels, in single-batch or multi-batch settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-benchmarking--639350f5a688927d))
- **single-cell-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when comparing a rare-cell or sub-cluster reclustering method against baseline scRNA-seq clusterers on datasets with known or suspected rare populations, e.g. PBMC3k or simulated pancreas data with rare closely placed clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-benchmarking--44844e4f6c1b7a09))
- **single-cell-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when comparing normalization/confounding-removal methods (e.g. scPLS vs RUV, LMM, PCA, scLVM) on scRNA-seq data with known cell labels, and you need ARI-based clustering performance per downstream method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-benchmarking--ec5122e3bf8bc197))
- **single-cell-clustering-eval**: Evaluates clustering algorithms on single-cell RNA sequencing data to assess their ability to separate distinct cell types and capture transitional states. It combines quantitative clustering metrics with visual validation using marker gene expression profiles. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Breast cancer dataset, Embryo neurone dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports misclustering rate. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/single-cell-clustering-eval) · ★3)
- **single-cell-clustering-for-parameter-estimation**: Use when fitting bursting kinetic parameters (kon, koff, s) from scRNA-seq counts with an MCMC/Gibbs sampler that assumes all cells share the same kinetics, and cells must first be divided by clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-for-parameter-estimation))
- **single-cell-clustering-interpretation**: Use when a trained scMM model's latent dimensions need biological interpretation, e.g. associating multimodal regulatory programs with individual latent dimensions via pseudocell traversal and TF motif enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-interpretation))
- **single-cell-clustering-metric-evaluation**: Use when translated or imputed single-cell profiles (e.g. scRNA↔scATAC cross-modality predictions) must be scored for preservation of cellular heterogeneity against ground-truth cell-type labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-metric-evaluation))
- **single-cell-clustering-of-calling-cards-data**: Use when analyzing single-cell Calling Cards qbed data (e.g., mouse cortex Brd4 scCC from GSE148448) where insertions must be assigned to cell types via Scanpy Leiden/Louvain clustering of matched scRNA-seq before differential peak analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-of-calling-cards-data))
- **single-cell-clustering-of-pbmc-transcriptomes**: Use when a gene-cell-barcode matrix from droplet scRNA-seq (tens of thousands of cells) must be partitioned into cell subpopulations and characterized by cluster-specific marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-of-pbmc-transcriptomes))
- **single-cell-clustering-partition-analysis**: Use when raw multimodal single-cell count data (RNA matrix X1 plus ADT matrix X2, e.g. h5 from GSE128639/bmcite) must be partitioned into unlabeled cell clusters and characterized by per-cluster marker/gene-set analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-partition-analysis))
- **single-cell-clustering-performance-evaluation**: Use when a trained scETM or p-scETM model has produced cell embeddings and you must quantify clustering quality (e.g., ARI/NMI on 'cell_types' labels), compare against competing integration tools, or check that a regularized variant is not compromised. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-performance-evaluation--2d3a57428d6f8343))
- **single-cell-clustering-performance-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a clustering or imputation method (e.g., scGCL, scTAG) on labeled scRNA-seq h5 data and you must quantify which KNN graph settings (k in {5,10,15,20,25}; city-block, cosine, euclidean, L2, manhattan distances) maximize ARI/NMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-performance-evaluation--fe120eca9d2230af))
- **single-cell-clustering-quality-scoring**: Use when many candidate clusterings exist (e.g. Totem's ~10k CLARA runs over a range of k) and an appropriate clustering must be automatically selected before constructing the trajectory MST. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-quality-scoring))
- **single-cell-clustering-result-annotation**: Use when scQA (or similar) clustering outputs exist for a scRNA-seq dataset and you must confirm that each cluster's key genes match known markers/DEGs to biologically annotate clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-result-annotation))
- **single-cell-clustering-robustness-quantification**: Use when you need to quantify the robustness or reproducibility of clusters produced by repeated stochastic single-cell clustering runs (e.g., SCA runs in rCASC), or when implementing cluster-stability validation metrics with a 0.5 explainability threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-robustness-quantification))
- **single-cell-clustering-sweep**: Use when benchmarking a pseudo-time reconstruction (e.g. consensus-TSP vs arbitrary insertion or Oscope) on cell-cycle-labeled scRNA-seq data across a sweep of cluster numbers k, needing correlation-scores per k. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustering-sweep))
- **single-cell-clustermining-with-autoencoder**: Use when scRNAseq counts (e.g. setA, 10XGenomics UMI tables) have pre-computed cell clusters and the goal is to discover which biological knowledge (TF, miRNA, IS, kinase targets) drives clusterization, or to test whether combining knowledge groups improves cluster reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-clustermining-with-autoencoder))
- **single-cell-cna-calling**: Use when analyzing PTA-amplified or low-coverage scWGS BAM files to call allele-specific CNAs, especially at small size scales (200 kb–5 Mb), or when comparing single-cell CNA callers on simulated or brain/meningioma datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cna-calling))
- **single-cell-cna-detection-from-scrna**: Use when scRNA-seq data (10x Genomics droplet-based or Smart-seq2) from a tumour sample must be profiled for allele-specific CNAs (copy gain, copy loss, copy-neutral LOH) and subclones reconstructed from sparse, low-coverage data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cna-detection-from-scrna))
- **single-cell-cnv-analysis-quality-control**: Use when preparing single-cell WGS (e.g., PTA- or MDA-amplified) data for CNA calling and an assay-specific minimum bin size must be chosen from measured allelic dropout, or when verifying that single-cell CNA calls pass QC thresholds (mappability, MAPQ, VAF filters). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cnv-analysis-quality-control))
- **single-cell-cnv-calling**: Use when starting from a scATAC-seq fragment count matrix (e.g., scDataSamp) and the goal is per-cell CNV inference, malignant/non-neoplastic cell classification, or linking genetic subclones to chromatin accessibility. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cnv-calling))
- **single-cell-cnv-calling-from-rnaseq**: Use when analyzing scRNA-seq data (e.g., 430-cell GBM dataset) to call large-scale CNVs per cell, identify mutually exclusive/co-occurring CNV subclones within a patient, and derive a CNV-based clonal phylogenetic tree.
