([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cnv-calling-from-rnaseq))
- **single-cell-cohort-representative-sampling**: Use when a cohort's bulk sequencing data (e.g., preprocessed COVID-19 cohort bulk RNA-seq from the scSemiProfiler example_data folder) must yield all representative samples in one batch, and multi-round active learning is impractical or would introduce batch effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cohort-representative-sampling))
- **single-cell-combinatorial-indexing-assay-annotation**: Use when you have a seqspec YAML for a combinatorial-indexing single-cell assay and need to identify where the cell barcode is encoded (e.g. Illumina index regions) before preprocessing FASTQs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-combinatorial-indexing-assay-annotation))
- **single-cell-communication-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq expression data (genes x cells) plus cell-type labels need unsupervised cell-cell communication inference without relying on predefined L-R pairs, e.g., non-human species or benchmarking against CellChat/CellPhoneDB/iTALK. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-communication-inference))
- **single-cell-communication-network-thresholding**: Use when building or visualizing a cell-to-cell communication network from single-cell expression data plus cell-type labels and you must decide which ligand–receptor edges pass detection (e.g. &gt;20% of cells), specificity, and expression cutoffs before drawing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-communication-network-thresholding))
- **single-cell-communication-quantification**: Use when you have scRNA-seq expression, cell-type labels, and (optionally) time-point annotations and need to infer and compare cell-cell interaction networks, especially to test how L-R aggregation schemes affect interaction recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-communication-quantification))
- **single-cell-community-flux-modeling**: Use when you have a patient-partitioned scRNA-seq object with cell-type annotations and want community-aware metabolic fluxes per cell type (e.g. glucose uptake across myeloid, B, T, epithelial cells) rather than per-cell or bulk fluxes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-community-flux-modeling))
- **single-cell-comparative-regulatory-analysis**: Use when you have four single-cell datasets (one scRNA-seq and one scATAC-seq from each of two conditions/populations) and need to identify subpopulation-specific differential regulatory relations between transcription factors and target genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-comparative-regulatory-analysis))
- **single-cell-confidence-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have an AnnData object with per-cell confidence scores from DNN training dynamics (e.g., mSTZ β cells annotated by condition) and want to rank genes by annotation-trainability association. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-confidence-correlation-analysis))
- **single-cell-copy-number-baseline-subtraction**: Use when you have a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (genes on rows, cells on columns) and need to remove the normal-cell expression baseline so that cell-specific copy number signal can be segmented, without manually identifying reference cell clusters as inferCNV requires. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-copy-number-baseline-subtraction))
- **single-cell-copy-number-calling**: Use when calling allele-specific somatic CNAs from paired single-cell + matched bulk WGS BAMs (PTA-amplified, ~20-30x scWGS), when small (200 kb-5 Mb) or subclonal CNAs must be resolved, or when benchmarking single-cell CNA callers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-copy-number-calling))
- **single-cell-copy-number-inference**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices from tumor biopsies (Smart-seq2 or 10X Chromium) must be automatically segregated into malignant and non-malignant cells and subcloned by copy-number architecture without a manually identified reference-cell cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-copy-number-inference))
- **single-cell-count-aggregation-by-cell-type**: Use when scUTRquant SingleCellExperiment objects contain sparse gene and 3'UTR isoform count matrices at single-cell resolution and counts must be summed per cell type before pairwise differential testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-aggregation-by-cell-type))
- **single-cell-count-data-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic scRNA-seq count matrices with known variance-component structure (random intercepts in mean and/or zero-inflation components) to evaluate type-I error or power of mixed-effects DE tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-count-data-simulation))
- **single-cell-count-data-simulation**: Use when benchmarking co-expression inference methods (e.g., CS-CORE vs Pearson, Spearman, locCSN, normalisr, NoiseRegularization) against a known true network, or when evaluating whether estimation bias harms downstream clustering/PCA via simulations with controlled n, p, and cluster structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-data-simulation))
- **single-cell-count-imputation**: Use when raw UMI-based scRNA-seq counts must be denoised/imputed prior to kinetic parameter estimation (kon, koff, s) with the RJAGS Gibbs sampler, or when scVI/MAGIC imputation is needed to meet modeling assumptions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-imputation))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-comparison**: Use when a long-read (Nanopore/PacBio) single-cell count matrix (e.g. Sicelore PREFIX_genematrix.txt) must be benchmarked against a same-sample Illumina Cell Ranger matrix to quantify agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-comparison))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-correlation-analysis**: Use when an alternative scRNA-seq quantification workflow must be benchmarked against a standard pipeline like Cell Ranger by comparing gene-level UMI count matrices across the same cells and genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-correlation-analysis))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-downsampling**: Use when you need to test stability of a fitted single-cell dynamical model (e.g. scKINETICS velocities) against technical noise in transcript capture by perturbing raw counts at reduced sequencing depths and refitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-downsampling))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-gene-filtering**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices must be converted into descending expression-ranked gene-symbol sequences for autoregressive transformer models like tGPT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-gene-filtering))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-generation**: Use when raw FASTQ files (or Cell Ranger BAMs convertible via bamtofastq) from any UMI-based scRNA-seq platform must be processed into gene-by-barcode matrices under one uniform pipeline, e.g. for cross-platform integration benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-generation))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-ingestion**: Use when the input is raw single-cell assay output such as a Cell Ranger filtered_feature_bc_matrix.h5 file, a matrix.mtx/features.tsv.gz/barcodes.tsv.gz directory, or a SnapATAC .snap file, and you need AnnData objects per modality for multimodal (e.g. RNA+ATAC) analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-ingestion))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw single-cell gene expression count matrix must be preprocessed to remove an annotated unwanted signal (batch, cell cycle, sex, spatial zonation, healthy-reference trajectory) before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing--529e04572e119fef))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., scEU-seq or BoolODE simulated data, genes x cells) are about to be fed into RNAForecaster and still contain high sparsity, non-log-transformed counts, or Float64 values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing--a938590a3c3b73b2))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when pooled scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq droplets have been labeled with hashtag oligonucleotides and you need to load HTO and RNA count matrices, derive per-droplet detected-gene totals, and check HTO-vs-gene correlations before demultiplexing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing--0a7db6b2f55654c0))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw scRNAseq count matrix must be pre-processed into standardized residuals prior to SVD/CA — e.g., to address overdispersion of counts instead of applying log(x+1) followed by PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing--99def826b7d24816))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw gene-by-cell scRNA-seq count matrices (cells in rows, genes in columns, CSV or similar) must be normalized and rescaled before scGATE inference, or when base GRN .parquet files need read_base_GRN() summarization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing--2a144cff51d9cee3))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix must be converted into a normalized, feature-selected expression matrix (anndata/Scanpy) as input to a graph-based clustering pipeline such as scMGCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing--c487867256c234ac))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw per-individual UMI count matrices from 10x Chromium scRNA-seq need gene filtering, top-1000 HVG selection, and marker-based approximated ground-truth cell-type labels before clustering benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-preprocessing--a6fa92798f85bed0))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-processing**: Use when HIT-scISOseq PacBio HiFi reads (post cellBC/UMI correction) must be converted into cell-by-gene or cell-by-isoform count matrices, or when cross-platform agreement with 10x Genomics/NGS counts on the same cDNA needs to be measured. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-processing))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-processing**: Use when a raw cells × genes scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., Tabula Muris '00_facs_raw_data/FACS/{tissue}-counts.csv') must be converted to a numeric numpy array and paired with a signal-free null dataset for empirical hypothesis testing of embedding quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-processing--891defde34d8e970))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-processing**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., GSE57872 glioblastoma cells with batch and patient labels) must be modeled without preliminary normalization, especially when batch and biology are partially confounded and covariates like detection rate must be included. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-processing--a578a95c338aefdf))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-quality-control**: Use when raw 10x Genomics single-cell or multiome (RNA+ATAC) count matrices (h5/CSV) must be filtered and normalized (log CPTT for RNA, CLR for ADT) before Leiden clustering and scTriangulate integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-quality-control--6e09bf379da87eb3))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-quality-control**: Use when starting from a raw scRNA-seq count matrix downloaded from GEO (e.g., GSE113197 breast epithelial cells) that must be filtered for low-quality cells and genes and normalized before clustering or marker-based cell annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-quality-control--79a3858683e58dcc))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-quality-control**: Use when raw spliced/unspliced gene-by-cell count matrices (e.g. from scKB/kallisto-bustools) need quality filtering, doublet removal, and downstream Seurat analysis before cell-type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-quality-control--65fe100517a7c6e2))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-simulation**: Use when benchmarking integration or differential-state detection methods needs synthetic two-condition, two-batch single-cell count data with known injected DS genes at controlled logFC (1.1–2) and prevalence (5% and 10%). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-simulation))
- **single-cell-count-matrix-smoothing**: Use when single-cell expression data (Scanpy AnnData .h5ad) is too sparse/noisy for per-cell gene set scoring, and a smoothed neighbor-averaged matrix is needed before applying methods like singscore or ssGSEA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-matrix-smoothing))
- **single-cell-count-modeling**: Use when modeling scRNA-seq gene-level count distributions (UMI or non-UMI) with heavy tails and point mass at zero, and you need platform-adaptive fitting with automatically estimated gene-specific index parameter p. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-modeling))
- **single-cell-count-normalization**: Use when a raw single-cell count matrix (e.g. a Seurat object or SingleCellExperiment such as the untreated PDAC snRNA-Seq dataset SCP1089) must be log-normalized with size factors and variable features scaled before clustering or LEADER subsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-normalization--338ce0b944431b29))
- **single-cell-count-normalization**: Use when an AnnData h5ad (e.g. the Macosko Mouse Atlas, 4.4M cells) is too large for in-memory Scanpy QC, and you need cell filtering, library-size normalization, and log transformation via AnnSQL SQL operations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-normalization--b8fb7261796d0c67))
- **single-cell-count-normalization**: Use when a raw UMI count matrix (e.g., from GEO GSM4905334) must be library-size normalized to the clone's median UMI count before signature scoring, filterGenesFano gene selection, or Geary's C autocorrelation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-normalization--08721542599ccbac))
- **single-cell-count-normalization**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count data (e.g., CELLxGENE census 2023-05-15 extracts) must be converted to the scTab sf-log1p representation — 10,000-count size factors plus log1p over 19,331 protein-coding genes — before model training, augmentation-vector computation, or inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-normalization--d421e868480d1e7e))
- **single-cell-count-preprocessing-for-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking gene network inference methods on raw single-cell count matrices: each method demands different preprocessing (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-preprocessing-for-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-count-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic scRNA-seq counts with known co-expression modules (ground-truth cluster labels) to benchmark correlation metrics or clustering pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-count-simulation))
- **single-cell-count-simulation**: Use when you need ground-truth scRNA-seq count data with known celltype labels and an applied dropout model to quantify whether a denoiser (DCA vs. an MSE autoencoder) recovers the true cell structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-count-simulation))
- **single-cell-cpm-count-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (.csv or .parquet) has already been quality-filtered in a scSPARKL/Spark pipeline and cell counts must be made comparable across cells prior to gene selection, PCA, and clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cpm-count-normalization))
- **single-cell-crispr-grna-cell-assignment-interpretation**: Use when single-cell CRISPR screen (CROP-seq) data has gRNA assignments labeled as intact/spacer-mutant/scaffold-mutant and you must confirm functional impact of gRNA mutations on target gene downregulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-crispr-grna-cell-assignment-interpretation))
- **single-cell-crispr-screen-association-testing**: Use when analyzing UMI read count + gRNA assignment matrices from a pooled single-cell CRISPR screen (e.g., high-MOI CROP-seq/Perturb-seq with thousands of gRNAs) to compute unbiased gRNA-gene associations with logFC, P values, and Q values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-crispr-screen-association-testing))
- **single-cell-crispr-screen-cell-filtering**: Use when preprocessing single-cell CRISPR screening data (Perturb-seq/CROP-seq expression profile plus perturbation information) to remove invalid-edit cells and low-cell-count perturbations before model building. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-crispr-screen-cell-filtering))
- **single-cell-crispr-screen-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw input is a gene-by-cell expression profile (e.g. expression_profile.txt) plus per-cell perturbation labels (perturb_information.txt), or 10X barcodes/genes/matrix files with a cbc_gbc_dict, and cells must be QC'd, imputed, and filtered before perturbation ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-crispr-screen-data-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-crispr-screen-quality-control**: Use when a single-cell CRISPR screen (e.g. CROP-seq) gRNA library BAM must be quality-controlled for mutant gRNAs and cells must be assigned gRNAs mutation-aware, beyond Cell Ranger feature barcoding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-crispr-screen-quality-control))
- **single-cell-crispr-screening-perturbation-ranking**: Use when you need to validate that a single-cell CRISPR screening pipeline's perturbation-effect ranking (e.g., MUSIC's Rank_overall) is stable under control-cell label noise, or to reproduce the reported robustness figures before trusting downstream ranking-driven conclusions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-crispr-screening-perturbation-ranking))
- **single-cell-cross-modality-translation**: Use when raw count AnnData matrices from a single modality must be translated into another (scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq peaks, or ADT), including unseen cell types, batch-shifted data, or chained multi-modality inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cross-modality-translation))
- **single-cell-cytometry-data-handling**: Use when you have single-cell data (cells x markers, e.g. CyTOF) with a priori class labels (cell type, cell-cycle phase, time point) and need an interpretable, non-stochastic low-dimensional visualization that maximizes class separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-cytometry-data-handling))
- **single-cell-data-augmentation-with-cell-type-labels**: Use when the number of paired training cells for scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq (or scADT-seq) translation is limited and clustering quality of translated profiles must be preserved, especially in five-fold down-sampling designs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-data-augmentation-with-cell-type-labels))
- **single-cell-data-format-conversion**: Use when a processed scRNA-seq object (Seurat or AnnData) must be made loadable in CellexalVR, which requires exported CellexalVR input files rather than native R/Python object formats. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-data-format-conversion))
- **single-cell-data-integration-benchmarking**: Decision support for choosing a homologous-gene mapping and integration algorithm when integrating scRNA-seq datasets across species, especially when species divergence time, number of species, or one-to-one vs in-paralog homology strategies may affect integration quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-data-integration-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-data-io**: Read, write, and create single-cell data objects using Seurat (R) and Scanpy (Python). Use for loading 10X Genomics data, importing/exporting h5ad and RDS files, creating Seurat objects and AnnData objects, and converting between formats. Use when loading, saving, or converting single-cell data formats. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/data-io) · ★1)
- **single-cell-data-value-sketching**: Use when a large scRNA-seq atlas (.h5ad, tens of thousands to millions of cells) must be subsampled before downstream ML/DL tasks such as cell-type annotation (scANVI, scPoli, CellTypist, ACTINN), label transfer, CellHint harmonization, or bulk deconvolution, and uniform or geometric sketching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-data-value-sketching))
- **single-cell-dataset-alignment**: Use when multiple annotated scRNA-seq datasets must be combined to learn a hierarchical classification tree, and batch effects are expected between the datasets (e.g., different PBMC studies measured on different platforms). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dataset-alignment))
- **single-cell-dataset-benchmark-table-construction**: Use when a cell can belong to multiple inferred trajectories and you need to quantify whether its inferred pseudotime is consistent across those paths, benchmarked per dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dataset-benchmark-table-construction))
- **single-cell-dataset-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking the scalability of a reference-mapping pipeline (e.g. Symphony/Harmony) and you need matched downsampled expression/metadata subsets at multiple cell counts as controlled inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dataset-downsampling))
- **single-cell-dataset-label-matching**: Use when a single-cell count matrix needs a biologically interpretable, cell-type-separating embedding: e.g., the MCA dataset must be embedded via the 137 marker GEPs and benchmarked against PCA, scDHA, or scMGCA using Davies-Bouldin and Calinski-Harabasz scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dataset-label-matching))
- **single-cell-dataset-subset-sampling**: Use when benchmarking execution time of single-cell methods across growing sample sizes: subset a raw cell-by-gene count matrix at fixed increments and record single-core wall-clock time and failures per method per size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dataset-subset-sampling))
- **single-cell-de-audit**: Review completed multi-donor single-cell differential expression before submission. Accept existing DE tables, sample sheets, AnnData, scripts and claims; produce located findings, evidence gaps, repairs and human review. Record pilot observations without claiming validated benefit. ([HERRY423/BioNexus](https://github.com/HERRY423/BioNexus/tree/HEAD/plugins/bionexus/skills/single-cell-de-audit) · ★32 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-deg-method-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare DEG prediction methods on single-cell RNA-seq data with known ground truth — i.e., artificial datasets with planted DEGs where AUC and elapsed runtime per method per dataset size can be tabulated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-deg-method-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-deg-visualization**: Use when comparing groups within annotated single-cell clusters or cell types, running Seurat FindMarkers by cell type, detecting pseudoreplication risks, exporting merged DEG tables, selecting top marker labels, or creating multi-cluster volcano plots such as jjVolcano figures. ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-deg-visualization))
- **single-cell-demultiplexing-preprocessing**: Use when preparing an (HTOs x droplets) count matrix plus per-droplet detected-gene counts for regression mixture model demultiplexing, and initial class labels or outlier flags are needed before model fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-demultiplexing-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-denoising-method-benchmarking**: Use when a sparse single-cell expression matrix with known cell-phenotype labels (e.g., 95% sparse PBMC or mouse cortex data) must be denoised and you need to verify whether RMT feature selection outperforms MAGIC, scImpute, ZIFA, ZIMB-WaVE, scVI, DCA, Seurat, or HVG baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-denoising-method-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-detection-rate-thresholding**: Use when filtering ligand-receptor pairs in single-cell expression data so a gene counts as 'expressed' in a cell type only if detected in more than 20% of its cells (NATMI --detectionThreshold default 0.2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-detection-rate-thresholding))
- **single-cell-diagonal-integration**: Use when you have unpaired single-cell modality pairs (e.g. scRNA-scATAC, scRNA-protein, scRNA-smFISH, scRNA-morphology) from the same biological condition and need a shared cell embedding without converting features across modalities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-diagonal-integration))
- **single-cell-differential-abundance-analysis**: Use when two annotated single-cell RNA-seq datasets processed in SingCellaR (e.g., eFL and FL HSPC objects with existing UMAP embeddings and cell-type labels) must be compared for cell-subpopulation abundance shifts, and per-cell DA scores plus a DA UMAP figure are the desired outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-abundance-analysis))
- **single-cell-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when you have a per-cell metabolomics matrix (e.g. MORPHnMOL.csv) with condition metadata and need to rank features by abundance shift between two cell populations, such as inflammatory vs benign steatotic states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-differential-abundance-testing))
- **single-cell-differential-activity-prediction**: Use when you have scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq, spatial transcriptomics, or trajectory data in h5ad/Seurat form with an embedding or coordinate space (PCA, pseudotime, 1D-3D spatial) and need clustering-independent detection of features with non-random activity patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-activity-prediction))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when a normalized single-cell RNA-seq matrix contains two or more condition groups (e.g. TGF-β-treated vs untreated HUVECs undergoing EndoMT) and a per-gene significance list is needed as input for regulatory-element queries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-analysis))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when you have per-cell cluster assignments (e.g. from CHAI-AvgSim or CHAI-SNF spectral clustering) and need marker genes per cluster to biologically annotate clusters, such as finding the CDH3-enriched tumor cell cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-analysis))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq count data or DE summary statistics (per-gene beta coefficient and variance) are available and gene-level differential expression calls between cell types or conditions are needed, especially when power or cross-method consistency is a concern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-analysis--7746724de7b84667))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq cells have been partitioned by a binary event (e.g., presence/absence of a fusion such as TMPRSS2-ERG) and you must test association with treatment or differential gene expression to quantify the fusion's transcriptional impact per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-analysis--5395d35beb81d2cd))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when you have partitioned droplet-based scRNA-seq barcodes into cell-calling groups (e.g. All-retained, EmptyNN-retained, EmptyNN-removed) and need to test whether a cell-caller's retained barcodes are transcriptionally similar to confirmed cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-analysis--4d2ccd5d9bbd142a))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-benchmarking**: Use when comparing DE methods on UMI or non-UMI scRNA-seq count data (synthetic via Splatter templates from Islam et al. 2011 and Klein et al. 2015, or published GEO datasets) to measure power and FDR control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-filtering**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset has been clustered (e.g., with scPlant/Seurat) and you need cluster-specific marker genes as input for promoter motif-enrichment analysis such as PMET on SCENIC regulons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-filtering))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-testing**: Use when a clustered single-cell AnnData object needs cluster-level marker/DE genes — e.g. to assign a cell type to a Leiden cluster via enrichment of its top differential genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-testing--12b0a3479fed9852))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-testing**: Use when annotated single-cell RNA-seq data with malignant/non-malignant labels is available and a robust, dataset-independent gene feature set (e.g., TMGs/NMGs) is needed for downstream cell classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-testing--15a22c6d7356f474))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-testing**: Use when a user defines two or more cell groups over a single-cell gene expression matrix and marker genes must be ranked by statistical significance to build a clustered heatmap or DE table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-testing--39db08a4a7c4d09c))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-testing**: Use when you have a gene-by-cell expression matrix with cell type/cluster assignments and need to identify cell type-specific DE genes or TFs (e.g., Welch's t test p &lt; 0.05) to feed signature or regulatory-network analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-testing--6a3443a8ef729f7e))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-testing**: Use when you have a library-size-normalized, log-transformed scRNA-seq expression matrix with cell cluster labels and need per-cluster marker ligand/receptor statistics (log2FC, adjusted P) to feed cell-cell communication inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-testing--7eaf392396a56ac9))
- **single-cell-differential-expression-testing**: Use when comparing two conditions of a single-cell dataset (e.g., IFN-stimulated vs. control PBMCs) and you need per-cell-type significance of ligand/receptor perturbation before scoring differential signaling edges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-expression-testing--2dd7c9a431a587dc))
- **single-cell-differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a log2-normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix with cluster labels and need to interpret clusters biologically via DE genes, GO terms, and KEGG pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-gene-expression-analysis))
- **single-cell-differential-gene-expression-testing**: Use when comparing scRNA-seq imputation methods on biological interpretability: you have raw and imputed expression matrices for the same cells and want to test whether imputation yields clearer DEG patterns and better recovery of disease-related KEGG pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-gene-expression-testing))
- **single-cell-differential-regulation-testing**: Use when sc_compreg has produced empirical likelihood ratio scores for TF-TG differential regulation and you need calibrated p-values and FDR control for them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differential-regulation-testing))
- **single-cell-differentiation-trajectory-analysis**: Use when single-cell gene expression data (e.g. ME, MMP, MFG, or MH datasets) must be ordered along pseudotime and multiple bifurcation events of cellular differentiation need to be detected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-differentiation-trajectory-analysis))
- **single-cell-dimension-reduction**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count data (cells × genes matrix) must be compressed to a low-dimensional embedding for visualization, clustering, or as input to classification or pseudo-time inference, especially with high dropout and technical noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimension-reduction--603a67fb9a50ef98))
- **single-cell-dimension-reduction**: Use when reducing dimensions of raw scRNAseq count matrices (SingleCellExperiment objects) to embed cells for clustering and cell-type recovery, and standard log(x+1)+PCA pipelines distort count data or overdispersion is a concern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimension-reduction--5d753344b85fa7c1))
- **single-cell-dimension-reduction**: Use when you have a temporal scRNA-seq gene-by-cell count matrix with a cell-by-time-point binary time table and need a 2D visualization that preserves developmental trajectories and resolves intra-time-point subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimension-reduction--fca2eaa86f8c80bd))
- **single-cell-dimension-reduction**: Use when a learned cell-to-cell similarity matrix from SIMLR's multi-kernel optimization is available and a low-dimensional embedding is needed to visualize and interpret cell populations in scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimension-reduction--37ebfb982525ed89))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when single-cell data (e.g., CyTOF markers, scRNA-seq PCs) come with a priori class labels such as cell type, cell-cycle phase, or experimental time point, and the goal is a supervised visualization whose axes separate those labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction--44a2ce1be16a9385))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix (wide format, e.g. tens of thousands to millions of cells from 10x Genomics data) exceeds single-machine memory and you need Spark-native PCA cell embeddings compatible with downstream Parquet-based UMAP/t-SNE steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction--2b67d55d0c559c72))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when an annotated single-cell Seurat object must be subsetted to a compartment (e.g., T/NK cells from the 12 liver transplant samples) and re-embedded/re-clustered via PCA -&gt; UMAP and graph-based clustering to reproduce reported cluster counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction--e0f8ab409ef85d9b))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-and-clustering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., ZF1_F3_DrImpute) must be converted into cluster labels and state lineages that downstream lineage-tree methods like LinRace NJ_asym_Dif require as inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-and-clustering))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking**: Use when comparing feature-reduction/embedding methods for scATAC-seq (or similar sparse single-cell) cell-type clustering, where robustness to per-cell coverage must be quantified with ARI/NMI against known labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking**: Use when assessing whether a dimensionality-reduction method can handle a million-plus-cell scRNA-seq dataset within practical runtime, by timing ivis against Barnes-Hut t-SNE with a 24-hour timeout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking--e6d9339a3263de8a))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking a supervised graph-embedding visualization (e.g. GraphDDP) against unsupervised and pseudotime baselines on single-cell data with prior cluster labels, to show which pathways baselines miss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking--6b1c8d1c8d57aa1b))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-coordinate-handling**: Use when dense dimension-reduction plots (UMAP/tSNE/DDRTree of scRNA-seq data, e.g. &gt;30k cells) must support freehand per-cell selection or per-point raycast hit detection in Unity, and attaching native colliders to every point is infeasible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-coordinate-handling))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-embedding**: Use when a QC-filtered, normalized scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., the Ind4 breast epithelium sample from GEO GSE113197, genes as rows and cells as columns) must be projected to low-dimensional space (PCA, then UMAP) before graph-based clustering and marker-based cell annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-embedding))
- **single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-pca-lsi**: Use when you need a low-dimensional cell representation of a single-cell count matrix (scRNA counts, protein ADT, or scATAC peak matrix) to feed a kNN graph, integration method, or label-consistency metric like kNN agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dimensionality-reduction-pca-lsi))
- **single-cell-disentangled-latent-model-training**: Use when a multi-batch scRNA-seq dataset contains cells measured under multiple biological conditions and you need to jointly remove batch effect while preserving condition effect in latent factors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-disentangled-latent-model-training))
- **single-cell-distance-metric-benchmarking**: Use when you need to validate that a proposed cell-to-cell similarity/distance measure captures same-cell-type affinity more strongly than a conventional pipeline baseline (e.g., HVG+PCA Euclidean distance), by quantifying deviation from a permutation null. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-distance-metric-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-distance-metric-computation**: Use when quantifying how closely inferred (semi-profiled) single-cell data matches real-profiled ground truth, or when scoring per-sample inference difficulty Dsc to drive active-learning representative selection on a bulk+single-cell cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-distance-metric-computation))
- **single-cell-dna-rna-cell-assignment-stability-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq and scDNA-seq cells from the same samples have been assigned with MaCroDNA but no paired ground truth exists, and you need statistical significance and stability of those assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dna-rna-cell-assignment-stability-analysis))
- **single-cell-dna-rna-count-matrix-handling**: Use when paired scDNA-seq copy-number and scRNA-seq expression matrices (e.g. the CRC dataset from Bian et al.) must be prepared as gene-by-cell dataframes for MaCroDNA cell-association or runtime/memory benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dna-rna-count-matrix-handling))
- **single-cell-dna-rna-integration**: Use when you have independent single-cell DNA copy-number and RNA expression matrices from the same sample (genes as rows, cells as columns) and need to associate RNA cells to DNA cells or infer cell-to-clone assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dna-rna-integration))
- **single-cell-donor-based-data-splitting**: Use when training or evaluating cross-tissue cell type classifiers on multi-donor scRNA-seq corpora where test cells must come from donors never seen during training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-donor-based-data-splitting))
- **single-cell-doublet-detection**: Detect and remove doublets (multiple cells captured in one droplet) from single-cell RNA-seq data. Uses Scrublet (Python), DoubletFinder (R), and scDblFinder (R). Essential QC step before clustering to avoid artificial cell populations. Use when identifying and removing doublets from scRNA-seq data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/doublet-detection) · ★1)
- **single-cell-doublet-ensemble-scoring**: Use when droplet-based scRNA-seq data may contain heterotypic doublets that destabilize downstream clustering or trajectory inference, and single-method doublet scores vary across datasets — an ensemble (GBM-boosted) score is needed before filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-doublet-ensemble-scoring))
- **single-cell-downstream-analysis**: Checklist-style reference for OmicVerse downstream tutorials covering AUCell scoring, metacell DEG, and related exports. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-downstream-analysis) · ★3.1k · 15 copies)
- **single-cell-downstream-fidelity-evaluation**: Use when a computationally inferred or semi-profiled single-cell cohort must be validated against a real-profiled cohort by comparing downstream outputs such as marker genes, GO enrichment, CellChat interaction matrices, and Monocle3 pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-downstream-fidelity-evaluation))
- **single-cell-dropout-event-modeling**: Use when reconstructing the decoder of the scMGCA graph convolutional autoencoder, or when a count-matrix generative loss must account for high-probability dropout events without assuming a zero-inflated negative binomial model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dropout-event-modeling))
- **single-cell-dropout-imputation**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix has extensive simulated or observed dropouts (e.g. 0.1%–10% sampling efficiency) and accurate imputation is needed before clustering, visualization, or pseudotime analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dropout-imputation))
- **single-cell-dropout-modeling**: Use when pre-training or adapting a single-cell classification model on raw scRNA-seq count matrices and a loss must capture the mean-dispersion-dropout structure (zero-inflation) of gene expression counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dropout-modeling))
- **single-cell-dropout-probability-estimation**: Use when an scRNA-seq raw count matrix contains dropout-driven false zeros and you need to estimate which gene-cell entries are affected by dropout before imputation, rather than altering all expression values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-dropout-probability-estimation))
- **single-cell-drug-repositioning**: Use when scRNA-seq data from diseased tissue (e.g., TNBC PDX models or patient samples like GSE123926/GSE161529) needs FDA-approved drug recommendations that account for multiple heterogeneous cell clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-drug-repositioning))
- **single-cell-drug-response-inference**: Use when you have untreated disease-state scRNA-seq data with cell-type annotations, a known drug target gene (e.g. Smo for vismodegib, Ctsk for tanshinone IIA), and need to rank which cell types are most drug-responsive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-drug-response-inference))
- **single-cell-drug-response-prediction**: Use when you have a clustered scRNA-seq AnnData object (scanpyobj.h5ad) and need cluster-wise drug sensitivity estimates (IC50) and cell-kill percentages for GDSC drugs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-drug-response-prediction))
- **single-cell-embedding-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq count data must be clustered and interpreted through topics — e.g. identifying topics differentially expressed between disease and control conditions or across cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-analysis))
- **single-cell-embedding-benchmarking**: Use when comparing how well alternative low-dimensional embeddings of simulated scRNA-seq preserve cell-cell topological structure, evaluated as trajectory (pseudotime) recovery measured by Kendall correlation against a known ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-benchmarking--f6375b9fc39f1cd8))
- **single-cell-embedding-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a single-cell embedding method (e.g., DIRECTi, DCA, PCA, tSNE) preserves cell-type resolution in scRNA-seq latent space, by computing MAP of nearest-neighbor cell retrieval between query and reference datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-benchmarking--060a0fe93bdc1824))
- **single-cell-embedding-clustering**: Use when inferring a tree-shaped trajectory from single-cell data and a diverse set of candidate clusterings is needed to construct minimum spanning trees and estimate cell connectivity, as in the Totem workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-clustering))
- **single-cell-embedding-clustering-accuracy-assessment**: Use when comparing batch-correction methods (e.g., scCobra, Seurat, Harmony, Scanorama, scVI, scDML, scDREAMER) on integrated single-cell embeddings where ground-truth cell-type labels exist and you need quantitative clustering/bio-conservation and batch-mixing metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-clustering-accuracy-assessment))
- **single-cell-embedding-clustering-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell embeddings/clusterings from multiple single-cell multi-omics integration methods and need quantitative embedding (silhouette, purity) and clustering (ARI across resolutions) metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-clustering-evaluation))
- **single-cell-embedding-distance-computation**: Use when cell embeddings (e.g. adata.obsm['scace_emb']) and cluster labels are available and you must quantify cluster separation by size-weighted inter-centroid distance to decide which clusters to merge or when merging stops. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-distance-computation))
- **single-cell-embedding-extraction**: Use when you need comparable cell embeddings from multiple single-cell foundation models (scPRINT, scGPT, Geneformer, scFoundation) for a downstream benchmark such as batch-effect correction scored by scIB avgBIO. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-extraction))
- **single-cell-embedding-extraction-transformer**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices or per-cell gene expression rankings must be converted into fixed-length cell embeddings for clustering, UMAP visualization, or batch-effect benchmarking without explicit normalization or batch correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-extraction-transformer))
- **single-cell-embedding-hyperparameter-optimization**: Use when a Seurat scRNA-seq object needs a t-SNE or UMAP 2D visualization and the hyperparameters (perplexity, min.dist, n_neighbors) are unvalidated, or when dubious cell embeddings must be detected before interpreting 2D cluster layout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-hyperparameter-optimization))
- **single-cell-embedding-quality-assessment**: Use when a t-SNE or UMAP visualization of single-cell data must be quality-controlled or its hyperparameters (perplexity, min.dist, n.neighbors) chosen objectively, e.g. when cluster adjacency or distances in the 2D plot need to be judged trustworthy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-quality-assessment--9dc4e9781d992fb1))
- **single-cell-embedding-quality-assessment**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq or scATAC-seq count matrix (e.g. Tabula Muris marrow FACS counts, 10x PBMC scATAC peak matrix) and need to judge which parts of a t-SNE/UMAP embedding reflect biological signal versus distortion, or to choose embedding hyperparameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-quality-assessment--feaa7241bcb8f6c0))
- **single-cell-embedding-quality-classification**: Use when a 2D scRNA-seq embedding (UMAP, t-SNE, diffusion maps) must be judged fit for trajectory/pseudotime inference, or when a UMAP run exhibits disconnected or unexpectedly grouped small clusters that would bias Slingshot/Monocle3 pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-quality-classification))
- **single-cell-embedding-quality-evaluation**: Use when comparing a novel embedding (e.g., GRN module AUCell scores) against batch-correction baselines on annotated snRNA-seq data, to quantify batch mixing vs retention of annotations like diagnosis, sex, or batch/sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-quality-evaluation))
- **single-cell-embedding-reliability-scoring**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq count matrix (Seurat object) and want to detect dubious 2D cell embeddings from t-SNE/UMAP, or to grid-search perplexity / min.dist / n_neighbors by minimizing the number of dubious cell embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-reliability-scoring))
- **single-cell-embedding-silhouette-evaluation**: Use when comparing dimensionality-reduction/visualization quality of scRNA-seq embeddings (e.g., scMGCA latent space vs PCA, t-SNE, UMAP) on datasets with ground-truth cell-type labels, quantified by average silhouette width (ASW). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-silhouette-evaluation))
- **single-cell-embedding-transfer**: Use when a processed SingCellaR object already contains Harmony/UMAP embeddings and Louvain cluster labels, and trajectory or pseudotime ordering (e.g. HSC/MPP to erythroid differentiation) is needed in Monocle3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-transfer))
- **single-cell-embedding-visualization**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix needs a 2D embedding and visualization to compare which method (EDGE vs. t-SNE vs. UMAP) preserves cell-type structure and separates rare populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embedding-visualization))
- **single-cell-embeddr-pvalue-estimation**: Use when a dimensionality-reduced single-cell embedding (t-SNE/UMAP of scRNA-seq or scATAC-seq counts) needs per-cell statistical quality assessment, hyperparameter (perplexity/kEff) optimization, or separation of biological signal from embedding artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-embeddr-pvalue-estimation))
- **single-cell-encode**: Find and work with ENCODE single-cell genomics data including scRNA-seq and scATAC-seq. Use when the user asks about single-cell experiments, cell type resolution, clustering from ENCODE data, deconvolution of bulk signals using single-cell references, or comparing single-cell vs bulk profiles. Covers platform differences (10X Chromium, Smart-seq2, Drop-seq), quality limitations of single-cell data, multimodal integration (RNA+ATAC), and cross-study reproducibility concerns. Also use for cell type annotation, gene detection limits, dropout artifacts, and single-cell data structure in ENCODE. ([ammawla/encode-toolkit](https://github.com/ammawla/encode-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/single-cell-encode) · ★20 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-entropy-based-cell-ranking**: Use when you have scRNA-Seq or bulk RNA-Seq expression data and need to rank cells/samples by differentiation potency or detect cancer stem-cell/drug-resistant phenotypes without marker genes or timepoint labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-entropy-based-cell-ranking))
- **single-cell-epigenomic-data-ingestion**: Use when raw single-cell epigenomic data (paired-end FASTQ or per-cell BED files from GEO accessions like GSE124680, GSE124690, GSE105012) must be converted into count matrices for ChromSCape-based subpopulation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-epigenomic-data-ingestion))
- **single-cell-epigenomics-peak-analysis**: Use when you have preprocessed single-cell ATAC-seq fragment files or count matrices and need to identify open chromatin regions (peaks) to support downstream differential accessibility analysis, motif discovery, or regulatory network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/single-cell-epigenomics-peak-analysis))
- **single-cell-error-rate-comparison**: Use when multiple cell type identification methods must be compared on spatial transcriptomics data with known ground-truth single-cell labels, especially to test whether joint multi-slice analysis improves accuracy over slice-by-slice analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-error-rate-comparison))
- **single-cell-expression**: Discover single-cell RNA-seq datasets and studies across public repositories -- CZ CELLxGENE Discover, Broad Institute Single Cell Portal, and DISCO (immunesinglecell.org). Use when identifying datasets that contain expression data for a tissue, cell type, or disease of interest, when checking whether single-cell data exists for a specific organ or cell population, or when planning experimental validation that requires cell-type-resolved expression context. Do not use for measured tissue-level expression (use tissue-expression-profile), for predicted regulatory variant effects (use regulatory… ([GoogleCloudPlatform/LifeSciences](https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/LifeSciences/tree/HEAD/applications/open-agentic-science-engine/skills/single-cell-expression) · ★58)
- **single-cell-expression-aggregation**: Use when a per-cell scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., cortex_data from Yao 2021) must be collapsed into group-level expression per cell subclass so ligand and target abundances can be scored against NeuronChatDB interaction entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-aggregation))
- **single-cell-expression-clustering**: Use when analyzing scywalker long-read single-cell (10x Oxford nanopore or PacBio) data and you need expression-based cell groups, cell-type assignments, and per-cell-type pseudobulk gene/isoform count tables per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-expression-clustering))
- **single-cell-expression-cpm-normalization**: Use when preparing a raw single-cell UMI count matrix (e.g. Tabula Muris or Tabula Muris Senis) as input to NATMI's ExtractEdges.py, which expects CPM-normalized expression profiles with a cell-annotation metafile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-cpm-normalization))
- **single-cell-expression-data-property-quantification**: Use when you have a real reference scRNA-seq/SRT expression matrix plus simulated counterpart matrices and need to score which data properties the simulator reproduces, as in the Simpipe accuracy module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-expression-data-property-quantification))
- **single-cell-expression-denoising**: Use when a sparse scRNA-seq or dropout-simulated count matrix needs denoising/imputation, or when comparing DCA vs MAGIC/SAVER/scImpute recovery of known gene associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-denoising))
- **single-cell-expression-dotplot-visualization**: Use when comparing expression of selected genes across multiple scRNA-seq studies whose cell annotations must be aligned to a shared standardized cell-type vocabulary (CellO) and a concise cross-study overview plot is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-dotplot-visualization))
- **single-cell-expression-imputation**: Use when raw or dropout-affected scRNA-seq count matrices (h5 files with expression values and true labels) need imputation to improve downstream clustering (ARI/NMI) or gene-expression recovery under synthetic dropout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-imputation))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-analysis**: Use when inferring TF activities from single-cell expression with a promoter binding-site linear model that is under-determined/overfits, and cell-to-cell smoothing over a kNN gene-expression graph is needed to control model complexity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-analysis))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-construction**: Use when starting from CellRanger/STAR BAM files from scRNA-Seq and you need locus-specific TE expression counts appended to the gene expression matrix for downstream single-cell analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-construction))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-loading**: Use when a log-transformed single-cell expression matrix from a tumor microenvironment sample must be reduced to independent gene-expression components whose loading scores are comparable across components and datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-loading))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-normalization**: Use when a raw CSV single-cell expression matrix (e.g. prot_expression.csv) with a class-labels CSV must be normalized per-cell before computing a correlation-coefficient matrix for graph-embedding layout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-normalization--068c35e10060acbb))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. Zheng_8ct_PBMC from DuoClustering2018, or GEO FACS-sorted datasets like GSE130430/GSE103544) must be normalized and gene-aligned before feeding a supervised annotation tool such as NeuCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-normalization--5a8788d676a72015))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a sample-by-feature single-cell expression matrix (e.g., PBMC or MCA gene expression counts in a .h5ad-like matrix X) must be normalized, reduced to ~30 PCs, and converted to a KNN affinity graph before clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing--10709839879d4808))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw single-cell read counts (e.g. an .h5ad/HDF5/loom matrix from scRNA-seq) must be reduced into pseudo-bulk representative cells before GCN inference, because single-cell expression is too sparse for WGCNA-style correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing--0ac7732363aeb952))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a large scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., 254,941-cell umbilical cord blood or 665,858-cell mouse brain data) must be converted into the CSV cell-by-feature/PC matrix format that Sphetcher requires before sketching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing--2961dbc8afd2adcd))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when a raw (genes x cells) scRNAseq count matrix (e.g. PBMC or Tabula Sapiens) must be prepared for q-diffused kernel construction, qPHATE, NMF/GEP extraction, or LDS, and the analysis goal requires retaining the full gene set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-preprocessing--4f096d6929255250))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when you have a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix and want per-cell PPIN activity per biological process, then Pearson-correlate those BP activity profiles across cells to see whether BPs cluster into distinct or anti-correlated groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-processing--424c6a8ea0c9511c))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-processing**: Use when scRNA-seq target cells have been clustered into sub-clusters and each must be written as an expression matrix file (e.g. data_obj/AML1_cluster1, 100 cells x 4K genes) for downstream counterpart mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-processing--c143495cca354384))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-qc-normalization**: Use when a raw gene-by-cell count matrix (e.g. from the MEF barcoding dataset GSE99915 of Biddy et al.) must be QC'd and normalized into a rows=genes, columns=cells matrix before lineage prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-qc-normalization))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-querying**: Use when a single-cell scRNA-seq study (e.g. the blood_covid demo, imported as H5AD) is loaded in Cellenium and you need the genes whose expression correlates with a query gene across all cells of that study. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-querying))
- **single-cell-expression-matrix-subsetting**: Use when validating predicted cell–cell communication by visualizing a specific gene's expression in two selected cell types from a spatial dataset (Seq-Scope/seqFISH+ .zip archives containing expression matrices and spatial coordinates).
