([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-matrix-subsetting))
- **single-cell-expression-normalization**: Use when a raw UMI count matrix (gene rows × cell columns) must be converted into a normalized, log1p-scaled expression matrix before running Cancer-Finder, CopyKAT, SCEVAN, CaSee, or ikarus on scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-normalization))
- **single-cell-expression-normalization-and-hvg-selection**: Use when a raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrix (.h5ad or GEO counts) must be quality-filtered, TPM-normalized, log1p-transformed, and reduced to ~2000 highly variable genes before DELVE feature-module identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-normalization-and-hvg-selection))
- **single-cell-expression-preprocessing**: Use when a raw cell-by-gene scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., .h5 or similar with X, Y, gene and cell names) must be filtered, normalized, and reduced to PCA/KNN/Louvain I-clusters before rare cluster detection or anomaly scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-preprocessing--09b3e71f0eac3812))
- **single-cell-expression-preprocessing**: Use when single-cell count matrices must be normalized, transformed, or batch-corrected before building 4D communication tensors, and robustness of inferred CCC patterns to preprocessing choices needs to be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-preprocessing--274666707e819150))
- **single-cell-expression-probability-model-fitting**: Use when fitting/validating scPower's expression probability priors on a new atlas count matrix (e.g., Drop-seq, Smart-seq2, 10X) to verify predicted expressed gene numbers match observed counts before power analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-probability-model-fitting))
- **single-cell-expression-resampling-bootstrapping**: Use when estimating a transition-driving GRN from time-stamped cross-sectional (TSCS) single-cell data and you need to guard the interaction matrix against imprecision in estimated sample mean/covariance moments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-resampling-bootstrapping))
- **single-cell-expression-subsampling**: Use when ICELLNET communication scores are computed from scRNAseq cluster mean profiles and you must quantify how score variability scales with the number of cells subsampled from a cluster (e.g. rare clusters, low cell counts). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-subsampling))
- **single-cell-expression-subsetting**: Use when a marker-gene panel (e.g. scGeneFit-selected genes) has been computed and you need to reduce full scRNA-seq expression data to just those gene columns before downstream visualization such as t-SNE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-subsetting))
- **single-cell-expression-thresholding**: Use when you need to determine the neighborhood size K for a nearest-neighbor graph, or when a binary 'how many geneset genes are expressed past a threshold' diagnostic per cell is needed for scRNA-seq AnnData data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-thresholding))
- **single-cell-expression-trajectory-simulation**: Use when benchmarking pseudotime inference accuracy on data with a known latent trajectory, especially to quantify how robust methods are when the fraction of genes with covariate-pseudotime ('pst-beta') interactions varies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-expression-trajectory-simulation))
- **single-cell-feature-embedding-analysis**: Use when tGPT feature embedding matrices plus cell type annotations (e.g. HCA bone marrow n=282,588 or HCL 586,135 cells) are available and the goal is pseudo-temporal developmental lineage inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-feature-embedding-analysis))
- **single-cell-feature-extraction**: Use when you need to reduce a large scRNA-seq gene-expression matrix (anndata .h5ad) to its most informative top-K genes for cell-type or clinical-condition classification, especially on resource-limited hardware. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-feature-extraction))
- **single-cell-feature-extraction-from-gene-rankings**: Use when input is ranked gene symbol lists per cell (top-expressing genes) rather than an expression matrix, and you need cell embeddings for clustering, batch-invariant integration, or UMAP visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-feature-extraction-from-gene-rankings))
- **single-cell-feature-importance-attribution**: Use when you have a pretrained Pollock module and annotated scRNA-seq data, and need to know WHICH genes drive a cell type/state classification, e.g. to feed top-ranked DWGs into GO enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-feature-importance-attribution))
- **single-cell-feature-prevalence-filtering**: Use when a single-cell SCMS peak matrix (e.g. imzML-derived peak lists of thousands of cells) contains sparse or noise-only m/z features, and you need prevalence-based pruning before clustering or cross-annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-feature-prevalence-filtering))
- **single-cell-flow-cytometry-density-modeling**: Use when you have an nx1 vector of pseudotime values (from Wanderlust, DPT/Diffusion Maps, etc.) and an nx1 vector of marker signals from n single cells and need the joint density p_s(s,y) on a regular grid as MAPiT input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-flow-cytometry-density-modeling))
- **single-cell-foundation-model-geneformer**: Use this skill when a task involves Geneformer workflows, especially TranscriptomeTokenizer input preparation, tokenized `.dataset` generation, cell or gene classification with `Classifier`, embedding extraction with `EmbExtractor`, and in silico perturbation analysis with `InSilicoPerturber`. - **single-cell-foundation-model-langcell**: Use this skill when a task involves the LangCell project for single-cell language-cell modeling, especially zero-shot cell type annotation, few-shot annotation, LangCell-CE finetuning, Geneformer-style tokenization, or preparing text descriptions for candidate cell identities and multimodal cell-text matching workflows. - **single-cell-foundation-model-scgpt**: Use this skill when a task involves the local scGPT project in /DATA/disk0/zhaosy/home/scGPT, especially scGPT preprocessing and binning, checkpoint vocabulary matching, cell embedding extraction, reference mapping, fine-tuning scGPT for integration or annotation, or using scGPT tutorials for GRN, perturbation, multiomics, and reference mapping workflows. - **single-cell-foundation-model-stofm**: Use this skill when a task involves the local SToFM project in /DATA/disk0/zhaosy/home/SToFM, especially preprocessing spatial transcriptomics data for SToFM, generating cell embeddings with the cell encoder plus SE(2) Transformer pipeline, handling spatial coordinates, or preparing SToFM embeddings for downstream region segmentation or cell type annotation. - **single-cell-functional-activity**: Use when analyzing single-cell functional activity scores such as DoRothEA/VIPER transcription factor activity, PROGENy pathway activity, GSVA/ssGSEA/AUCell/scMetabolism metabolic pathway scores, or summarizing activity matrices by cell type, sample, tissue, or condition. - **single-cell-gan-generator-architecture**: Use when building or modifying the generator of a single-cell GAN (scGAN/cscGAN) so that generated cells carry realistic library sizes, or when validating that a normalization layer is differentiable in TensorFlow. - **single-cell-gene-and-cell-filtering**: Use when the input is a raw (unfiltered, unnormalized) digital gene expression matrix (rows = genes, columns = cells), e.g. from Cell Ranger, that will feed scTITANS or a trajectory-based differential expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-and-cell-filtering))
- **single-cell-gene-barcode-count-matrix-generation**: Use when raw FASTQ files from a droplet scRNA-seq platform (e.g. SRA SRP073767) must be converted into a per-cell UMI count matrix with barcode/UMI correction and PCR-duplicate removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-barcode-count-matrix-generation))
- **single-cell-gene-co-expression-network-inference**: Use when a large single-cell count matrix (&gt;5000 cells recommended) needs gene-level co-expression network construction and identification of co-expression modules (GEPs), especially where weak co-expression signals and dropouts defeat Pearson-correlation approaches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-co-expression-network-inference))
- **single-cell-gene-count-matrix-generation-from-umis**: Use when uniquely mapped FLNC reads with corrected cellBC/UMI assignments and a filtered tmap already exist, and a per-cell x per-gene count matrix is the required final deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-gene-count-matrix-generation-from-umis))
- **single-cell-gene-count-quantification**: Use when raw paired FASTQ reads from a single-cell scRNA-seq library (e.g. 10xv2/10xv3 chemistry) must be converted into a gene-by-cell sparse count matrix (Matrix Market or AnnData .h5ad) from an existing splici/piscem index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-count-quantification))
- **single-cell-gene-embedding-training**: Use when you have single-cell RNA-seq count data (AnnData/.h5ad) and need low-dimensional gene embeddings or unsupervised metagene discovery of co-regulated transcriptional programs across conditions or timepoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-embedding-training))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-count-matrix-generation**: Use when starting from raw 10x-style GEX and/or ADT FASTQ files (I1/R1/R2, SAMPLENAME_S[Number]_L00[Lane]_[ReadType]_001.fastq.gz) and per-sample gene or antibody count matrices are needed before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-count-matrix-generation))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-decomposition**: Use when seq-based ST spots (e.g., ~55 μm Visium spots) contain multiple cells and per-cell whole-transcriptome expressions must be reconstructed using a labeled scRNA-seq reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-decomposition))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when the input is a raw single-cell or tabular gene expression matrix (e.g., scRNA-seq count data, Slide-seq locations, BDTNP dge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-feature-scoring**: Use when the RMT eigen-decomposition of a single-cell covariance matrix has already been partitioned into noise-bulk and signal-outlier eigenvectors and you must rank individual genes by signal-like variance (e.g., to select ~1,500 genes as the paper does). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-feature-scoring))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-filtering**: Use when benchmarking or reproducing gene-expression filtering runtime on atlas-scale (&gt;memory) single-cell datasets, comparing AnnSQL on-disk SQL queries versus AnnData backed-mode subsetting on a laptop-class machine. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-filtering))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-modeling**: Use when the goal is to reproduce or verify the fixed architecture of Pollock's VAE-based cell-type classifier (encoder/decoder, 64-dim latent, classification head, combined loss) against a pretrained Zenodo checkpoint before reuse or retraining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-modeling))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-reconstruction**: Use when benchmarking how well 50 markers selected by unsupervised methods (unsupervised MarkerMap, RandomBaseline, Scanpy HVGs, unsupervised PERSIST) reconstruct full transcriptome expression from CITE-seq, Mouse Brain, Paul, or Zeisel scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-reconstruction))
- **single-cell-gene-expression-trend-verification**: Use when a filtered single-cell expression matrix (e.g. SiFT-filtered GSE145197 liver data) must be checked for preservation of a target temporal trend while an unwanted signal (spatial zonation) is eliminated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-expression-trend-verification))
- **single-cell-gene-fusion-detection**: Use when Smart-Seq single-cell FASTQ files (*_1.fastq, *_2.fastq) must be screened for gene fusions, e.g. recovering known fusions (IgH-WHSC1, TMPRSS2-ERG, TCR V(D)J recombinations) or benchmarking against bulk fusion callers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-fusion-detection))
- **single-cell-gene-list-interpretation**: Use when you have RMT-selected signal and discarded gene lists from a single-cell dataset (e.g., Zeisel mouse cortex, Kang PBMC) and need to test whether the selected genes correspond to tissue-specific biological processes rather than generic ones. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-list-interpretation))
- **single-cell-gene-network-inference**: Use when the input is a single-cell read count matrix (e.g., Smart-seq2 or GEO deposits like GSE114727, GSE75748, Tabula Muris) and the goal is to infer an undirected, sparse gene co-expression network robust to excess zero counts/dropout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-network-inference))
- **single-cell-gene-perturbation-clustering**: Use when a scRNA-seq matrix has been split into training and imputable data (via hyper-geometric dropout testing) and genes must be partitioned into subspaces for subspace regression imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-perturbation-clustering))
- **single-cell-gene-regulatory-network-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq data has been normalized, scaled and Louvain-clustered and the goal is to identify transcription-factor regulons whose activation is cluster-specific, e.g. reproducible Fig. 2G-style regulon activity heatmaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-regulatory-network-inference))
- **single-cell-gene-set-density-analysis**: Use when you have a cell-by-gene scRNA-seq matrix (Seurat object) and curated gene sets, and need to determine whether specific gene sets are heterogeneously coordinated across cells without clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-set-density-analysis))
- **single-cell-gene-set-scoring**: Use when per-cell gene set scores on Scanpy AnnData (h5ad) scRNA-seq data are needed and count sparsity/technical noise is degrading detection — e.g. comparing smoothed vs unsmoothed scores or testing whether smoothing (K=4/8/32) improves separation of cell states or treatment labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-set-scoring))
- **single-cell-gene-signature-marker-extraction**: Use when a labeled reference scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. Drop-seq clusters) must be converted into per-cluster marker gene sets before scoring or classifying cells of an unlabeled target dataset (e.g. Smart-seq2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-signature-marker-extraction))
- **single-cell-gene-signature-prediction**: Use when the analysis goal is to derive cell type-specific signature genes from a clustered SINCERA R S4 expression object, once cell types have been identified and marker genes can be registered or must be inferred de novo. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-signature-prediction))
- **single-cell-gene-signature-scoring**: Use when you need per-cell enrichment scores for cell-type or process gene signatures from scRNA-seq data (matrix, Seurat, or SingleCellExperiment input) and require scores robust to dataset heterogeneity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gene-signature-scoring))
- **single-cell-genotype-matrix-processing**: Use when a detected variant/VAF matrix from scRNA-seq variant calling contains germline-annotated SNPs (e.g., ESP6500 frequency annotations) that must be removed before lineage reconstruction with SClineager. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-genotype-matrix-processing))
- **single-cell-genotype-phenotype-modeling**: Use when you have a binary SNV matrix (n_cells x n_SNVs) from scRNA-seq or coupled DNA-/RNA-seq single cells and a gene expression matrix (n_cells x n_Genes), and want expression-linked SNV features (eeSNVs) for subclustering or genotype markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-genotype-phenotype-modeling))
- **single-cell-genotyping-from-bam-pileups**: Use when a list of somatic SNVs from paired tumor/normal eWGS must be detected per-cell in 10x 5' scRNA-seq BAMs (CB/UB_tag barcodes), producing per-sample mutant-cell counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-genotyping-from-bam-pileups))
- **single-cell-graph-construction-neighborhood**: Use when you have preprocessed single-cell RNA-seq data (normalized and dimensionality-reduced via PCA) and need to establish cell-to-cell connectivity for trajectory inference, clustering validation, or graph-based visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/single-cell-graph-construction-neighborhood))
- **single-cell-graph-embedding-clustering**: Use when clustering raw or preprocessed scRNA-seq count matrices where preserving cell-cell topological relationships matters, e.g. datasets with rare cell types, small clusters, or &gt;25,000 cells needing batched learning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-graph-embedding-clustering))
- **single-cell-graph-embedding-layout**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq expression matrix plus prior cluster labels and need a 2D layout that shows cluster membership together with differentiation trajectories between clusters (e.g. stem-cell-centered lineages, branch points). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-graph-embedding-layout))
- **single-cell-grn-construction**: Use when building a lineage-specific GRN from a scRNA-seq expression matrix (e.g., AnnData with a developmental trajectory) and a prior gene interaction network edgelist, as input to driver regulator or gene module analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-construction))
- **single-cell-grn-construction-pc-regression**: Use when reconstructing gene regulatory networks from raw scRNA-seq count matrices (genes as rows, cells as columns) and you need a GRN construction method with high specificity and sensitivity across a wide range of cell numbers (10 to 3,000 cells). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-construction-pc-regression))
- **single-cell-grn-cross-tool-comparison**: Use when two gene regulatory network inference tools (e.g., MINI-EX and SCENIC) have been run on the same single-cell atlas and their cell-type-specific regulon outputs must be compared and validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-cross-tool-comparison))
- **single-cell-grn-driver-ranking-analysis**: Use when evaluating whether a method's ranked driver regulator list recovers known cell-fate regulators (e.g., NANOG, SOX2, POU5F1) against GO ground-truth sets in scRNA-seq benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-driver-ranking-analysis))
- **single-cell-grn-inference-and-ensemble-merging**: Use when the goal is to build a whole-transcriptome GRN from a scRNA-seq, snRNA-seq, or spatial dataset and the analysis requires robust consensus edges, module detection, and downstream perturbation-prediction evaluation (AUROC, TPR at FPR 0.05). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-inference-and-ensemble-merging))
- **single-cell-grn-network-visualization**: Use when you have pySCENIC/SCENIC GRN outputs from plant single-cell data and need to visualize which regulon TFs characterize specific cell clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-network-visualization))
- **single-cell-grn-perturbation-score-aggregation**: Use when scRNA-seq data has been clustered into pseudotime/Monocle states, CellOracle GRNs are inferred per state, and you need to rank TFs by predicted effect of knockout on a fate-transition process. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-perturbation-score-aggregation))
- **single-cell-grn-visualization**: Use when cluster-specific GRN Links objects must be scored and compared across a reprogramming or differentiation trajectory, and centrality/cartography structure (e.g., Hnf4α-Foxa1 edge strength, connector-hub detection) needs to be visualized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grn-visualization))
- **single-cell-grna-assignment-threshold-tuning**: Use when assigning gRNAs to cells from a Perturb-seq/CROP-seq gRNA capture library and you need mutation-aware cell-to-guide assignment with adaptive UMI thresholds rather than fixed ad hoc cutoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-grna-assignment-threshold-tuning))
- **single-cell-gwas-gene-prioritization**: Use when GWAS summary statistics and a normalized single-cell Seurat object are available and the goal is to rank trait-relevant genes and compare their functional enrichment to gene-scoring baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gwas-gene-prioritization))
- **single-cell-gwas-polygenic-regression-analysis**: Use when you have GWAS summary statistics (e.g., IEU ID ieu-b-2 AD GWAS) plus a cell-annotated snRNA-seq Seurat object (e.g., Grubman entorhinal cortex, 11,786 cells) and need trait-relevant cell types and single cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-gwas-polygenic-regression-analysis))
- **single-cell-h5ad-preprocessing**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset is available as an .h5ad file and you want TFvelo to fit TF-based gene dynamics, infer pseudotime/trajectory, and rank transcription factors on data lacking splicing information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-h5ad-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-heatmap-visualization**: Use when two or more cell groups have been defined in-session from single-cell expression data and a ranked marker-gene heatmap with per-gene Wilcoxon p-values and FDRs is required to compare groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-heatmap-visualization))
- **single-cell-hic-cell-quality-control**: Use when preparing raw single-cell Hi-C count data (e.g. oocyte vs zygote cells) for scHiCDiff DCI testing and you need to filter low-contact cells and restrict contacts to a short genomic distance range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hic-cell-quality-control))
- **single-cell-hic-contact-binning**: Use when starting from per-cell tab-separated single-cell Hi-C contact files (chromosome and mapped-position columns) that must be converted to binned contact matrices at 10-kb resolution prior to imputation or loop calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hic-contact-binning))
- **single-cell-hic-count-matrix-analysis**: Use when you have a normalized scHi-C count matrix (rows = bin pairs, columns = cells) plus a two-level condition factor and need per-bin-pair differential interaction statistics and BH-adjusted p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hic-count-matrix-analysis))
- **single-cell-hic-data-aggregation**: Use when you have single-cell Hi-C contact maps stored as cool files plus a bulk Hi-C map from the same processing pipeline, and need to aggregate cells into ensemble compartment eigenvectors and quantify agreement with bulk compartments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-hic-data-aggregation))
- **single-cell-hic-median-computation**: Use when comparing per-cell interaction complexity across single-cell Hi-C protocols from published valid-pair/contact files, especially when IVT-amplified libraries (sci-L3) must be deduplicated against restriction-fragment combinations to avoid inflated counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-hic-median-computation))
- **single-cell-hic-pairtools-pair-filtering**: Use when processing single-cell Hi-C (e.g. sci-L3-Hi-C) aligned pairs into valid interaction pairs, needing duplicate marking, UU/UR/RU pair selection, MboI fragment annotation, and valid-pair retention/duplicate-rate statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-hic-pairtools-pair-filtering))
- **single-cell-hic-tld-detection**: Use when raw (non-imputed) single-cell Hi-C contact matrices (e.g., Tan GM12878, Nagano mouse ES, Flyamer, Lee PFC datasets) need hierarchical TAD-like domain calls, and when benchmarking TLD predictors on sparse per-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hic-tld-detection))
- **single-cell-hidden-factor-detection**: Use when scRNA-seq count data appears homogeneous under known covariates (e.g., a single cell type within patients) but unexplained heterogeneity — such as doublets, cell-cycle stage, or outlier cells — is suspected and PCA/tSNE fails to separate it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hidden-factor-detection))
- **single-cell-hierarchical-clustering**: Use when a preprocessed, z-score transformed expression matrix of cell type-selective genes is available and cells must be assigned to major cell type clusters within the SINCERA pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hierarchical-clustering))
- **single-cell-hierarchical-count-modeling**: Use when a sparse droplet-based scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq count matrix (genes x cells, integer counts) has multi-subject structure (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hierarchical-count-modeling))
- **single-cell-hierarchy-progressive-learning**: Use when two or more annotated scRNA-seq datasets must be merged into a single hierarchical cell-population tree, especially when annotations differ in resolution (e.g., AMB2016 vs AMB2018 visual cortex) and new populations may need to be discovered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hierarchy-progressive-learning))
- **single-cell-hto-count-matrix-processing**: Use when you have a cell hashing HTO count matrix (e.g. an 8-hashtag dataset such as Stoeckius-2018 GSE108313) and need to assign singlets, doublets and negatives per cell before comparing demultiplexing methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-hto-count-matrix-processing))
- **single-cell-identity-scoring-against-reference-profiles**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data from a reprogramming or fate-conversion experiment must be scored per cell for source (fibroblast) versus target (iEP) identity to compare conditions, e.g. testing whether adding Fos/Yap1 to a cocktail increases target identity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-identity-scoring-against-reference-profiles))
- **single-cell-identity-validation**: Use when a reference-based classifier (e.g. scMAGIC first round) has produced candidate labels per query cell and you must separate confidently validated cells from low-confidence negative controls before retraining or reclassification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-identity-validation))
- **single-cell-immune-repertoire-object-manipulation**: Use when single-cell VDJ-seq data (SingleCellExperiment with TCR information) must be pseudobulked into a VDJ feature space of V/D/J gene usage counts per miloR neighbourhood for downstream trajectory analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-immune-repertoire-object-manipulation))
- **single-cell-imputation-benchmarking**: Use when you need to compare scRNA-Seq imputation/denoising methods quantitatively — e.g. simulated counts with known true values and group labels across noise types such as dropout, uniform, Gaussian, Gamma, Poisson, negative binomial. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-imputation-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-imputation-result-visualization**: Use when imputed single-cell expression matrices (hd5, log10(count+1) scale) are available and you need to visually assess whether an imputation method recovers nonlinear gene-gene relationships or compare methods against ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-imputation-result-visualization))
- **single-cell-imputed-matrix-comparison**: Use when you hold raw plus imputed log-transformed scRNA-seq matrices (e.g. Jurkat-293T, Usoskin, Kolodziejczyk, PBMC) with cell-type annotations and must rank imputation methods by variance stabilization within a cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-imputed-matrix-comparison))
- **single-cell-integration-benchmarking**: Use when you have per-cell UMAP embeddings from multiple integration methods plus batch and cell-type metadata and need to objectively compare batch mixing versus cell-type conservation across covariates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-integration-benchmarking--f61ded3266c435ff))
- **single-cell-integration-benchmarking**: Use when a reference-query single-cell label transfer or integration method must be ranked against alternatives on scIB metrics, e.g. over a multi-cancer AnnData split where some tools (Seurat-based, &gt;85 batches) may be inapplicable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-integration-benchmarking--11661b1a40d9a186))
- **single-cell-integration-latent-embedding-modeling**: Use when input is a multi-sample, multi-condition single-cell count matrix (e.g., sparse dgCMatrix) with sample/condition covariates, and you need an integrated latent embedding plus cluster-free DE or sample-variability deconvolution across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-integration-latent-embedding-modeling))
- **single-cell-integration-reproducibility-validation**: Use when reproducing or auditing how STACAS weights integration anchors and derives the guide tree that orders dataset integration, or when validating an independent implementation against Run.STACAS defaults. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-integration-reproducibility-validation))
- **single-cell-intrinsic-gene-detection**: Use when scRNA-seq count data across multiple batches has been fitted with BUSseq and you need the gene set that differentiates the discovered cell types at a controlled Bayesian FDR cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-intrinsic-gene-detection))
- **single-cell-isoform-count-summarization**: Use when the input is a filtered isoform-level single-cell count matrix (e.g. 6794 isoforms from Tasic et al. Smart-Seq2 data) with cell type labels, and sparse cell-level counts make direct cell-to-cell correlations too noisy for co-expression estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-isoform-count-summarization))
- **single-cell-isoform-count-verification**: Use when a collapsed long-read single-cell isoform FASTA (e.g. all.collapsed.rep.fa from cDNA_Cupcake) must be classified with SQANTI3 and the resulting per-sample retained isoform counts and structural-category distribution (FSM/ISM/NIC/NNC) need validation against reported values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-isoform-count-verification))
- **single-cell-isoform-downsampling-robustness-testing**: Use when you must assess how stable isoform-level DE/DIU calls are to random cell sampling in single-cell isoform count matrices, e.g. before trusting multi-group DESeq2/edgeR DIU results on Tasic-style neural Smart-Seq2 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-isoform-downsampling-robustness-testing))
- **single-cell-isoform-quantification-benchmarking**: Use when comparing accuracy of single-cell/spatial Nanopore isoform quantification pipelines on simulated data with known ground-truth cell-by-isoform expression matrices, across sequencing quality or depth regimes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-isoform-quantification-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-isoform-quantification-matrix-generation**: Use when long-read single-cell Nanopore data with 10x cell barcodes and UMIs must be quantified against known transcript annotations to produce per-cell isoform and gene count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-isoform-quantification-matrix-generation))
- **single-cell-isotope-tracing-data-analysis**: Use when MALDI imaging MS data paired with microscopy segmentation has been acquired after 13C6-glucose labelling and the goal is per-cell fatty-acid isotopologue distributions and uptake/synthesis fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-isotope-tracing-data-analysis))
- **single-cell-isotopologue-data-normalization**: Use when processing SpaceM single-cell MALDI imaging output (spatiomolecular adata per well) into quality-controlled, per-cell fatty acid isotopologue distributions ready for binomial labelling fits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-isotopologue-data-normalization))
- **single-cell-junction-matrix-quality-filtering**: Use when a raw junction reads matrix (junc_matrix.csv / junc_mat.npy) has been integrated LeafCutter-style from single-cell SMART-seq BAM or SJ.out.tab files and must be quality-filtered before AS event grouping and probability computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-junction-matrix-quality-filtering))
- **single-cell-kmeans-clustering**: Use when you have a wide-format normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix with a PCA-reduced representation and need unbiased cell clustering, optimal-k selection, and cluster-pair differential expression ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-kmeans-clustering))
- **single-cell-knee-plot-qc-filtering**: Use when analyzing 10x single-cell/nuclei Oxford nanopore (or PacBio) long-read cDNA and you need to call real cells via knee-plot thresholds plus emptydrops, or to build/interpret the scywalker HTML QC report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-knee-plot-qc-filtering))
- **single-cell-knn-graph-clustering**: Use when quality-filtered 10X scRNA-seq data (matrix.mtx, gene.tsv, barcode.tsv) with selected PCs needs high-resolution T cell cluster discovery, and cluster assignments must be validated against a shuffled-data null to avoid spurious clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-knn-graph-clustering))
- **single-cell-label-mapping-across-datasets**: Use when a target scRNA-seq dataset lacks cell labels but a reference dataset with known clusters exists, especially when batch effect or strong imbalance in cell numbers/library sizes makes integration-based or biomarker-based mapping unreliable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-label-mapping-across-datasets))
- **single-cell-label-quality-control**: Use when a training/reference single-cell dataset carries 'Malignant'/'Tumor' labels assigned by heterogeneous original-study methods, and you must calibrate or validate those labels before training a classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-label-quality-control))
- **single-cell-latent-activity-comparison**: Use when comparing gene module (pathway/TF/cell-type) latent activity between two cell groups in scRNA-seq data, e.g. stimulated vs control PBMCs, and you need per-module significance rather than eyeballing latent means. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-latent-activity-comparison))
- **single-cell-latent-embedding-clustering**: Use when the task is cell-type clustering on raw scRNA-seq count data (e.g. datasets like 'Adam') where deep-autoencoder latent representations must be learned and converted into consensus cell-type partitions evaluated by NMI/ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-latent-embedding-clustering))
- **single-cell-latent-embedding-training**: Use when per-modality low-dimensional representations must be produced and compared before manifold alignment, e.g. benchmarking topoAE against AE, VAE, and UMAP on PCA/LSI-reduced single-cell matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-latent-embedding-training))
- **single-cell-latent-representation-scoring**: Use when a source-free transfer learning pipeline needs per-cell uncertainty for target scRNA-seq cells from classifier outputs, e.g. to feed bimodality tests for novel cell-type detection in scEMAIL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-latent-representation-scoring))
- **single-cell-latent-space-clustering**: Use when temporal scRNA-seq data must be visualized on a single 2D latent space and subpopulations within one time point (e.g. E7.75 Cd41+ cells) need to be identified and marker-validated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-latent-space-clustering))
- **single-cell-latent-space-embedding**: Use when you have a normalized single-cell transcriptomic dataset (Scanpy AnnData) plus prior biological knowledge in GMT format and want each latent dimension to correspond to a named gene module rather than an opaque factor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-latent-space-embedding))
- **single-cell-ld-axes-visualization**: Use when cells from an unseen condition or subset must be visualized on axes learned from labeled training cells (e.g., projecting CD57high T cells onto CD57low-trained LD axes over day0–day5 TCR stimulation) to compare progression states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-ld-axes-visualization))
- **single-cell-leiden-clustering**: Use when a per-cell spatio-molecular matrix (e.g. MORPHnMOL.csv with 29,738 hepatocytes across +FA, +FA+IL17A, +FA+IL17A+TPCA1 conditions) must be partitioned into discrete metabolic states without labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-leiden-clustering))
- **single-cell-library-size-calculation**: Use when preprocessing raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., iasva_counts_test.Rds) and you need to decide which principal components or library-size covariates capture technical depth variation before surrogate-variable analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-library-size-calculation))
- **single-cell-library-size-normalization**: Use when raw single-cell gene expression counts (e.g., the featurecounts.qc slot of GBM10_preproc) have varying per-cell totals and must be depth-corrected before gene-level parameter estimation such as BPSC/SCALE bursting kinetics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-library-size-normalization))
- **single-cell-library-size-summarization**: Use when raw droplet 10x Genomics data (e.g. mouse epithelial cells from ArrayExpress E-MTAB-6854 sequenced on HiSeq 4000) must be reduced from all barcodes to called cells, with per-sample cell counts and library-size summaries reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-library-size-summarization))
- **single-cell-lineage-barcode-analysis**: Use when you have paired single-cell lineage barcode (character matrix) and scRNA-seq expression data with per-cell type labels, and need to reconstruct and biologically validate a cell lineage tree by comparing barcode-defined subtrees against transcriptomic cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-lineage-barcode-analysis))
- **single-cell-lineage-cluster-assignment**: Use when you have a single-cell phylogeny (Newick/phylo object with cells as leaves) and need to group cells into a target number of clade-coherent subpopulations, e.g. to define tumor subclones or lineage labels for signature-scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-lineage-cluster-assignment))
- **single-cell-lineage-prediction**: Use when you have a quality-controlled, normalized scRNA-seq gene expression matrix (genes x cells) and want to infer close lineage relationships (over ~1-5 cell divisions) without dedicated lineage-tracing assays, ideally with a barcode dictionary available for validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-lineage-prediction))
- **single-cell-lineage-subsampling-simulation**: Use when benchmarking differential trajectory-topology or cell-proportion tests on multi-sample scRNA-seq data by artificially removing cells from a known lineage and checking false positive rate at 0% removal and power as removal increases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-lineage-subsampling-simulation))
- **single-cell-lineage-tracing**: Reconstruct cell lineage trees from CRISPR barcode tracing or mitochondrial mutations. Use when studying clonal dynamics, cell fate decisions, or developmental trajectories. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/lineage-tracing) · ★1)
- **single-cell-log-normalization**: Use when raw UMI count matrices must be transformed to a common sequencing-depth scale via log(1 + scaled expression) — e.g., for exploratory visualization, marker plots, or when reproducing benchmarks of how downstream co-expression estimators behave on log-normalized data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-log-normalization))
- **single-cell-log-normalization-preprocessing**: Use when preparing a sparse scRNA-seq count matrix (rows=cells, columns=genes, CSV/JLD2 from 10x data) for RMT-based signal/noise separation, where raw log normalization would inflate detected signals (e.g., 84 vs 42 signals on ZhengMix) due to non-uniform cell vector lengths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-log-normalization-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-log2-tpm-preprocessing**: Use when raw unsynchronized scRNA-seq expression (e.g. TPM values in ola_mES_2i.RData or youngMpp .RData) must be converted to log2(TPM+1) and restricted to the cycle base genes to build reCAT's standard test_exp input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-log2-tpm-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-long-read-exon-quantification**: Use when you have full-length spliced long reads assigned to single cells/cell types and need cell-type-resolved exon usage quantification plus exon-wise differential exon usage testing between biological groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-long-read-exon-quantification))
- **single-cell-long-read-pipeline-qc-metrics**: Use when deposited subreads BAM files from a 10x Genomics + PacBio single-cell isoform experiment must be reprocessed through CCS and FLNC classification to verify reported per-sample QC metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-long-read-pipeline-qc-metrics))
- **single-cell-long-read-splicing-analysis**: Use when long-read FASTQs (PacBio/ONT) from single cells assigned to cell types must be converted into a cell-type-specific alternative splicing/DIE analysis, or when bulk DIE events must be traced to one vs. multiple cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-long-read-splicing-analysis))
- **single-cell-malignancy-classification**: Use when a tumor scRNA-seq expression matrix needs malignant/non-malignant cell labels but no confidential internal reference cells exist, or when comparing malignancy classifiers (XGBoost vs ElasticNet vs Neural Network). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-malignancy-classification--bcaee1219569cbbe))
- **single-cell-malignancy-classification**: Use when scRNA-seq .h5ad data from a known cancer lineage must be split into malignant vs normal epithelial cells without CNV inference, especially to track malignancy probability across progression stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-malignancy-classification--c9a14cde579322f6))
- **single-cell-malignancy-inference**: Use when you need to label individual cells as malignant or non-malignant in human tumor scRNA-seq data without a reference, especially across pan-cancer TISCH datasets or to benchmark annotation speed against CNV-based tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-malignancy-inference))
- **single-cell-manifold-alignment**: Use when two single-modality single-cell datasets (e.g. ATAC source, RNA target) share no overlapping cells or features and must be aligned into a common latent space for label-matching or integration scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-manifold-alignment))
- **single-cell-marker-based-cell-type-scoring**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq counts (AnnData) need standardized cell-type labels via reference-derived marker genes, especially for million-scale datasets on limited compute or when model-based methods (Seurat, Harmony, scNym, scArches) are too costly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-marker-based-cell-type-scoring))
- **single-cell-marker-based-loss-computation**: Use when training or reproducing BIDCell-style segmentation on SST data (e.g. Xenium transcripts.csv.gz + morphology_mip.ome.tif) and you need per-component and total biologically-informed loss values on real image patches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-marker-based-loss-computation))
- **single-cell-marker-gene-differential-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset has already been normalized, dimensionality-reduced and clustered (e.g. SF clusters on PBMC3k), and per-cluster marker genes are needed for cluster annotation or functional enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-marker-gene-differential-analysis))
- **single-cell-marker-gene-identification**: Use when you have a trained Miscell encoder on scRNA-seq data (e.g., Smart-seq HVG matrix) with cell cluster labels and need cluster-specific marker genes via attribution scores rather than differential expression tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-marker-gene-identification--72d8270c48a62075))
- **single-cell-marker-gene-identification**: Use when annotating cell types of scRNA-seq clusters automatically and you need cluster marker genes that are upregulated in exactly one cluster, avoiding pseudo markers (e.g. CCL22, SWAP70, KLRF1) that arise from one-against-all comparisons and impair annotation consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-marker-gene-identification--cda957b2b41b2a85))
- **single-cell-marker-selection-solver-execution**: Use when given a simulated or real scRNA-seq expression matrix with cell-type labels and a target marker panel size (e.g., 50 markers), and you must run scGeneFit to extract selected gene indices and measure solver runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-marker-selection-solver-execution))
- **single-cell-marker-weighted-annotation**: Use when single-cell or spatial RNA-seq data (Seurat object or raw/normalized count matrix) must be annotated with interpretable, consistency-weighted markers, or when disease-critical cell types such as leukemic stem cells must be scored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-marker-weighted-annotation))
- **single-cell-markers-annotation**: Find marker genes and annotate cell types in single-cell RNA-seq using Seurat (R) and Scanpy (Python). Use for differential expression between clusters, identifying cluster-specific markers, scoring gene sets, and assigning cell type labels. Use when finding marker genes and annotating clusters. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/markers-annotation) · ★1)
- **single-cell-matrix-factorization-usage-analysis**: Use when benchmarking matrix-factorization-based GEP discovery (e.g., cNMF) against network-based GEP identification (e.g., SingleCellGGM) on a labeled single-cell dataset, to quantify how many programs are cell-type-specific versus shared activity programs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-matrix-factorization-usage-analysis))
- **single-cell-matrix-serialization**: Use when a single-cell dataset stored as an AnnData (.h5ad) object or a Scanpy sample dataset must be converted into a persistent, SQL-queryable on-disk database so expression matrices and metadata can be joined and filtered with SQL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-matrix-serialization))
- **single-cell-mean-expression-aggregation-by-celltype**: Use when you must reproduce or de-novo compute the weight_norm edgeweight of every Connectome edge from per-celltype mean normalized expression, or when validating ligand×receptor product scoring on scRNAseq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-mean-expression-aggregation-by-celltype))
- **single-cell-mec-space-projection**: Use when you have a tumor scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., TISCH cohorts like BCC pre/post-ICB or BLCA responder/non-responder) and need consistent, interpretable cell-state scores rather than manual marker-based labeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-mec-space-projection))
- **single-cell-meta-component-interpretation**: Use when annotating data-driven single-cell gene programs (86 MetaTiME MeCs) with candidate transcriptional regulators using TF ChIP-seq binding evidence, or when validating TF prioritizations for tumor immune cell states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-meta-component-interpretation))
- **single-cell-metabolic-flux-estimation**: Use when a single-cell expression matrix (rows = genes, columns = cells, raw or normalized counts, e.g. Melissa_full.csv) needs cell-wise predicted metabolic flux and metabolite variation outputs from the human M171 module model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-metabolic-flux-estimation))
- **single-cell-metabolic-state-clustering**: Use when a per-cell spatio-molecular matrix (normalized single-cell metabolomes with microscopy-derived phenotypic properties) needs to be partitioned into metabolic states, or when assessing whether such state assignments are robust to cell-filtering choices such as excluding co-sampling ablation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-metabolic-state-clustering))
- **single-cell-metabolite-communication**: Analyze metabolite-mediated cell-cell communication using MeboCost for metabolic signaling inference between cell types. Predict metabolite secretion and sensing patterns from scRNA-seq data. Use when studying metabolic crosstalk between cell populations or metabolite-receptor interactions. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/metabolite-communication) · ★1)
- **single-cell-metabolomics-hierarchical-clustering**: Use when you have pretreated SCMS single-cell metabolomics feature matrices (e.g., primary/metastatic melanoma cell pairs, untreated or vemurafenib-treated) and want a label-free baseline check of phenotype separability before training a classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-metabolomics-hierarchical-clustering))
- **single-cell-metabolomics-matrix-preparation**: Use when a raw single-cell metabolomics dataset (e.g. METASPACE annotations or an .rds single-cell matrix) must be reshaped into a metabolites-by-cells input matrix with condition labels before running S2IsoMEr ORA/MSEA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-metabolomics-matrix-preparation))
- **single-cell-metacell-construction**: Use when you have a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. 10X/h5ad from GEO like GSE114953) and need to compress hundreds of thousands of single-cell profiles into ~75-cell metacells for downstream atlas-level analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-metacell-construction))
- **single-cell-model-guide**: Choose and sanity-check scvi-tools models such as scVI, scANVI or totalVI and read their training logs, integration metrics and errors. Use for single-cell integration, not general data analysis. ([Kukutx/superskills](https://github.com/Kukutx/superskills/tree/HEAD/plugins/science/skills/single-cell-model-guide))
- **single-cell-module-scoring**: Use when you have Hotspot-identified heritable gene modules (e.g., from phylogenetically autocorrelated genes in a lineage-traced tumor clone) and need a per-cell score for each module to overlay on phylogeny or expression projections. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-module-scoring))
- **single-cell-module-scoring-ssgsea**: Use when you have a single-cell expression matrix and defined gene signatures (meta-modules or cell-cycle sets) and need per-cell module scores plus dominant-module calls to annotate clusters from an unsupervised clustering such as RCC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-module-scoring-ssgsea))
- **single-cell-mosaic-multiomics-integration**: Use when a dataset contains multiple single-cell batches that jointly profile different subsets of modalities (scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq, CITE-seq protein, chromVAR motifs), especially with unequal cell-type composition across batches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-mosaic-multiomics-integration))
- **single-cell-mtdna-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking mtDNA variant-calling tools against known ground-truth clonal variants in simulated Smart-seq2-like scRNA-seq data, or when generating simulated single-cell mtDNA BAMs with controlled clone counts, allele frequencies, and lineage trees. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-mtdna-read-simulation))
- **single-cell-multi-batch-dataset-integration**: Use when concatenating multiple scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., Baron, Lawlor, Muraro pancreas datasets) with batch and cell-type annotations and you need joint low-dimensional embeddings that both preserve cell-type separation and integrate batches for comparison against PCA-based pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multi-batch-dataset-integration))
- **single-cell-multi-omic-model-fitting**: Use when paired transcriptome and surface protein (CITE-seq/REAP-seq ADT) data are available for training and the goal is to impute relative surface protein abundances from UMI-based scRNA-seq counts alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multi-omic-model-fitting))
- **single-cell-multi-omics-analysis-scvi**: Probabilistic deep learning framework for single-cell multi-omics data analysis. Use this skill when: (1) Analyzing single-cell RNA-seq data with batch correction, (2) Integrating multi-modal data (CITE-seq, ATAC-seq, multi-omics), (3) Performing cell type annotation with scANVI, (4) Spatial transcriptomics deconvolution with DestVI. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-multi-omics-analysis-scvi) · ★1.1k)
- **single-cell-multi-omics-data-harmonization**: Prepare your RNA-seq, proteomics, methylation, and other omics datasets for joint integration by applying per-assay normalization, cross-assay batch correction, feature ID alignment, and missing value handling. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-multi-omics-data-harmonization) · ★1.1k)
- **single-cell-multi-omics-integration**: Quick-reference sheet for OmicVerse tutorials spanning MOFA, GLUE pairing, SIMBA integration, TOSICA transfer, and StaVIA cartography. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-multiomics) · ★3.1k · 19 copies)
- **single-cell-multi-omics-preprocessing**: Use when paired single-cell modality matrices (e.g. DBIT-seq transcriptomic/proteomic or granulocyte scATAC-seq/scRNA-seq count matrices) must be preprocessed before training scMMGAN or its CycleGAN/CMAE baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multi-omics-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multi-table-correspondence-analysis**: Use when multiple scRNA-seq count tables (batches, samples, or platforms) sharing a common gene feature space must be integrated into a joint low-dimensional embedding, especially when PCA+Harmony shows poor batch mixing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multi-table-correspondence-analysis))
- **single-cell-multimodal-clustering-integration**: Use when you have multimodal single-cell data (e.g. CITE-Seq, Multiome, TEA-Seq) with multiple conflicting annotation sets from Leiden clustering at different resolutions, and need a reconciled per-cell population assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-clustering-integration))
- **single-cell-multimodal-data-integration**: Use when two single-cell omics matrices (e.g., snRNA-seq counts and snATAC-seq peak counts from the same study but unpaired barcodes, such as 10x Multiome PBMC data) must be co-embedded and cross-modal cell pairing is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-data-integration))
- **single-cell-multimodal-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw single-cell datasets with paired TCR sequences and gene expression must be annotated (cell type, tissue, cancer source) and filtered before training mvTCR or building a multi-study reference atlas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-data-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multimodal-deep-clustering**: Use when you need to jointly cluster RNA+ADT (CITE-seq) data and assess whether a learned latent space separates cell types while removing batch effects across batches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-deep-clustering))
- **single-cell-multimodal-embedding-analysis**: Use when you have paired CITE-seq data (RNA gene counts CSV + raw protein ADT counts CSV, rows = cells, columns = genes/proteins) and need joint cell embeddings or want to quantify complementary, modality-specific information across cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-embedding-analysis))
- **single-cell-multimodal-embedding-evaluation**: Use when you have paired scRNA-seq + TCR-seq data and need to quantify whether a multimodal latent embedding improves downstream prediction (antigen specificity, binding avidity from pMHC multimer counts) or cluster identification relative to unimodal baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-embedding-evaluation))
- **single-cell-multimodal-integration**: Analyze multi-modal single-cell data (CITE-seq, Multiome, spatial). Use when working with data that measures multiple modalities per cell like RNA + protein or RNA + ATAC. Use when analyzing CITE-seq, Multiome, or other multi-modal single-cell data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/multimodal-integration) · ★1)
- **single-cell-multimodal-matrix-io**: Use when input data are 10x Cell Ranger HDF5 (feature-barcode) matrices that must be loaded as multimodal MuData objects, split into modalities such as 'Antibody capture', and serialised to .h5mu for downstream processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-matrix-io))
- **single-cell-multimodal-trajectory-branch-subsetting**: Use when a single-cell multimodal dataset forms a tree-like or branching differentiation trajectory and you need to ablate topology — testing whether full-trajectory DAG inference or individual branch sub-DAGs explain the observed locus-gene signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-trajectory-branch-subsetting))
- **single-cell-multimodal-vae-inference**: Use when you have single-cell transcriptome count data (RNA_count.mtx with gene.tsv/barcode.tsv) from a CITE-seq experiment and need to predict paired surface protein abundances from a pretrained multimodal VAE, then quantify prediction accuracy against measured protein counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-vae-inference))
- **single-cell-multimodal-wnn-clustering**: Use when a droplet-based single-cell dataset contains matched mRNA and antibody-derived tag (ADT) measurements and you need a joint cell clustering whose dependence on protein normalization (e.g., dsb vs. CLR) must be evaluated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multimodal-wnn-clustering))
- **single-cell-multiome-count-preprocessing**: Use when starting from raw 10x Genomics multiome output (e.g. filtered_feature_bc_matrix.h5) that must be normalised per modality and feature-selected before multi-omics factor analysis or alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-multiome-count-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multiome-count-preprocessing**: Use when raw paired RNA-seq + ATAC-seq single-cell multimodal counts (e.g. 10x Multiome, SHARE-seq, SNARE-seq, sci-CAR) must be normalized and filtered before peak-gene link analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiome-count-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multiome-data-partitioning**: Use when you need held-out likelihood evaluation of batch-wise single-cell models (e.g., MIRA RP models on the 13-batch NEURIPS 10x Multiome bone marrow data) and must split cells per batch into train/test sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiome-data-partitioning))
- **single-cell-multiome-pseudobulk-aggregation**: Use when single-cell multiome ATAC+RNA counts need to be aggregated per cell type into pseudo-bulk profiles before computing gene-peak correlations, as in bootRanges multiomicsCor.R (14 pseudo-bulk cell types). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiome-pseudobulk-aggregation))
