- **single-cell-multiome-subpopulation-linking**: Use when you have four single-cell data sets (one scRNA-seq plus one scATAC-seq from each of two conditions, e.g. CLL donor vs healthy control) and need to identify and compare condition-specific subpopulations and their regulatory programs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiome-subpopulation-linking))
- **single-cell-multiomic-alignment-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when you have aligned single-cell embeddings from multiple alignment methods (e.g. scTopoGAN, UnionCom, MMD-MA) on paired-modality datasets (RNA/ATAC PBMC, RNA/ADT BM) and must rank them on clustering and cross-modality prediction performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomic-alignment-benchmark-evaluation))
- **single-cell-multiomic-cell-assignment**: Use when you have two independent single-cell datasets from the same sample — scDNA-seq copy-number profiles and scRNA-seq expression profiles — restricted to a shared gene set, and need a one-to-one cell correspondence or RNA-cell-to-DNA-clone mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomic-cell-assignment))
- **single-cell-multiomic-data-integration**: Use when a scRNA-Seq analysis has predicted primed cells and candidate regulatory factors (e.g. GATA1, KLF1, IRF8 in fetal liver MPPs) and matched scATAC-Seq data from the same cell types is available for epigenomic validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomic-data-integration))
- **single-cell-multiomic-joint-embedding**: Use when a per-cell feature matrix contains paired protein and metabolite intensity blocks of unequal feature size and you need a joint low-dimensional embedding to compare reconstruction against single-modality VAE baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-multiomic-joint-embedding))
- **single-cell-multiomic-peak-gene-linking**: Use when you have paired scATAC-seq/scRNA-seq data from a dynamic single-cell process and need to rank candidate chromatin peak-gene regulatory links by causal strength rather than correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomic-peak-gene-linking))
- **single-cell-multiomic-preprocessing**: Use when raw unprocessed RNA-seq and ATAC-seq AnnData objects from a paired single-cell multimodal assay must be normalized and filtered to define valid candidate peak-gene pairs for downstream GrID-Net or pseudotime analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomic-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multiomics**: Advanced single-cell multi-omics analysis including scRNA-seq, scCITE-seq, scATAC-seq, and TARGET-seq. Use when analyzing single-cell data, cell type identification, trajectory analysis, differential expression, UMAP/clustering, integrating protein and RNA modalities (TotalVI), or working with Scanpy, Seurat, scvi-tools. Includes workflows for MPN, hematologic malignancies, megakaryocyte biology. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/single-cell-multiomics) · ★104)
- **single-cell-multiomics-count-preprocessing**: Use when raw or minimally processed CITE-seq or SMAGE-seq h5 count matrices need per-modality normalization (mRNA vs. ADT/ATAC) before feeding a deep clustering model such as scMDC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-count-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multiomics-data-integration**: Use when you have paired single-cell multiomics count matrices (e.g. RNA + surface protein CITE-seq, or RNA + ATAC peaks) and need joint dimensionality reduction, cross-modal prediction, or interpretable latent dimensions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-data-integration))
- **single-cell-multiomics-data-preprocessing**: Use when input is paired transcriptome/proteome data in h5ad (anndata.X expression; spatial coordinates in anndata.obsm['spatial']) that must be normalized and dimension-reduced before neighborhood graph construction or multi-omics integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-data-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multiomics-data-simulation**: Use when benchmarking CITE-seq clustering methods (scMDC vs. TotalVI, Seurat V4 WNN, SC3, scDCC, BREM-SC, CiteFuse, Specter) requires controlled mRNA+ADT data with ground-truth labels, or for linear-runtime scalability tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-data-simulation))
- **single-cell-multiomics-generative-model-training**: Use when you have paired single-cell multiomics raw count matrices (RNA and ATAC or CITE-seq) in mtx format with gene/peak/barcode annotation files and need joint representations or crossmodal prediction between modalities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-generative-model-training))
- **single-cell-multiomics-integration**: Use when integrating unpaired annotated scRNA-seq (source, h5ad) with unlabeled scATAC-seq gene activity data (target, h5ad) for label transfer, atlas-scale joint embedding, or discovering scATAC-seq cell types absent from scRNA-seq annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-integration))
- **single-cell-multiomics-integration-training**: Use when paired transcriptome–proteome data are available as AnnData (.h5ad) objects and a joint low-dimensional embedding is needed for Leiden clustering and metric-based comparison against methods like MOFA+, Seurat v5, totalVI, or scMM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-integration-training))
- **single-cell-multiomics-preprocessing**: Use when preparing paired single-cell multi-omics data (e.g. 10X Multiome, CITE-seq, TEA-seq) for integrative methods like Mowgli: raw count matrices per modality need QC, normalization, and feature selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-multiomics-simulation**: Use when benchmarking or validating single-cell multi-omics integration, clustering, or GRN-inference methods requires synthetic scRNA-seq + scATAC-seq data with known differential expression, co-expression patterns, and regulatory ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiomics-simulation))
- **single-cell-multiplet-detection**: Use when an (HTOs x droplets) hashtag-oligonucleotide count matrix from pooled cells/nuclei must be assigned to samples of origin with error probabilities, uncertain-droplet discarding, and multiplet (singlet/doublet) detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-multiplet-detection))
- **single-cell-nanopore-error-correction**: Use when single-cell or spatially barcoded Nanopore long-read alignments suffer from read truncation, misaligned small internal exons, or sequencing-error-driven false-positive novel isoforms that must be corrected before isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-nanopore-error-correction))
- **single-cell-nearest-neighbor-graph-construction**: Use when preparing single-cell RNA-Seq count data for ELVAR differential abundance testing: a Seurat-processed count matrix with PCA is available and an igraph state-manifold with condition attributes is needed for cell-attribute aware community detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-nearest-neighbor-graph-construction))
- **single-cell-neighbor-preservation-evaluation**: Use when you have one or more 2D embeddings (e.g. UMAP, t-SNE, MDS on subsets of highly variable genes) of scRNA-seq data and must decide which embedding is suitable for pseudotime/trajectory inference before fitting Slingshot or Monocle3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-neighbor-preservation-evaluation))
- **single-cell-neighborhood-graph-construction**: Use when scRNA-seq data with a pre-computed RNA velocity field (e.g., scVelo on an AnnData object with cluster annotations) must be converted into a probabilistic cell-to-cell transition graph for fate/trajectory simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-neighborhood-graph-construction))
- **single-cell-neighbour-distance-processing**: Use when a scRNA-seq rare-cell workflow needs candidate minor-cluster sizes derived from an inter-cluster gap signal, i.e. a precomputed cell-to-neighbour distance matrix is available and k must be estimated via ∆∆D skewness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-neighbour-distance-processing))
- **single-cell-network-flow-entropy-quantification**: Use when scRNA-seq data along a differentiation trajectory (e.g. progenitors vs mature cells) must be ordered by potency of individual cells, using per-cell network entropy rather than expression-only scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-network-flow-entropy-quantification))
- **single-cell-network-inference-benchmarking**: Use when comparing gene regulatory/co-expression network inference methods on scRNA-seq count matrices, especially for quantifying robustness via half-sampling agreement and wall-clock runtime/memory on 10X or Smart-seq2 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-network-inference-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-noise-signal-decomposition**: Use when a sparse single-cell expression matrix (e.g. scRNA-seq counts with ~95% zeros) must be split into noise, sparsity-driven artifacts, and true biological signal without assuming i.i.d. noise distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-noise-signal-decomposition))
- **single-cell-omics-dataset-acquisition-and-preprocessing**: Use when a task requires downloading a public multimodal single-cell dataset (TEA-seq/CITE-seq RNA + ADT) and preprocessing it into the features-by-cells modality format expected by SnapCCESS before embedding generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-omics-dataset-acquisition-and-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-online-label-transfer**: Use when new scRNA-seq query datasets with batch effects must be annotated against an existing reference atlas, and retraining a model or re-fitting the classifier for each arriving dataset is impractical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-online-label-transfer))
- **single-cell-ontology-based-annotation**: Use when annotating single-cell RNA-seq data against a controlled vocabulary (Cell Ontology), especially when the query data may contain cell types absent from the training annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-ontology-based-annotation))
- **single-cell-overdispersion-decomposition**: Use when fitting per-gene mixed models to multi-subject scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq counts and you need to separate between-subject from within-subject overdispersion, or test whether covariates (e.g., age, sex, AD status) explain subject-level variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-overdispersion-decomposition))
- **single-cell-pacbio-read-qc-filtering**: Use when processing raw PacBio subreads from single-cell PAIso-seq libraries and you need quality-filtered, adapter-trimmed CCS reads with reliable poly(A) tail calls before isoform or tail-length analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-pacbio-read-qc-filtering))
- **single-cell-pathogen-quantification-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the computational cost (CPU/wall-clock time) of pathogen quantification tools—scPathoQuant, ViralTrack, PathogenTrack, Venus—on an identical 10x scRNAseq sample to rank them from fastest to slowest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathogen-quantification-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-pathogen-read-alignment**: Use when you have 10x scRNAseq data already processed with cellranger count and need to detect, quantify, and differentiate viral or bacterial pathogen reads per cell in a single operation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathogen-read-alignment))
- **single-cell-pathway-activity-comparison**: Use when you have scRNA-seq expression matrices from two populations (e.g. healthy vs disease, blood vs tissue) and need to rank pathway perturbations by distributional change rather than enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathway-activity-comparison))
- **single-cell-pathway-activity-scoring**: Use when you must compute PAS for a large pathway collection (&gt;10^3 gene sets) across a large scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., 100k+ cells, million-cell atlases) and existing scorers like UCell, AUCell, or AddModuleScore are too slow or memory-hungry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathway-activity-scoring))
- **single-cell-pathway-distribution-testing**: Use when comparing pathway activity between scRNA-seq conditions (e.g. resting vs 24h-activated naive CD4+ T cells) and you need distribution-level perturbation signals, not gene-set over-representation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathway-distribution-testing))
- **single-cell-pathway-dysregulation-analysis**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq expression matrix (genes x cells, log2(TPM+1)-style), cell-level metadata with 1/0 condition labels, a contrast file, and you need to identify which signaling pathways are dysregulated per cell type across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathway-dysregulation-analysis))
- **single-cell-pathway-perturbation-analysis**: Use when comparing pathway activity across multiple conditions, time points, or pseudotime phases in scRNA-seq data — especially when enrichment-based methods miss pathways with distributional shifts but little fold change. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathway-perturbation-analysis))
- **single-cell-pathway-score-visualization**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset has been quality-controlled and cell-annotated and you need to quantify a signaling pathway (e.g., angiogenesis) per cell, rank cell types by score, and highlight pathway-high subpopulations in the tumor microenvironment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pathway-score-visualization))
- **single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when preparing synthetic or real single-cell expression data (e.g., dyntoy datasets of ~1000 cells) for trajectory inference, where methods like VIA consume low-dimensional PC embeddings rather than full gene-expression matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction--a75657f7098ddef3))
- **single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when two (or more) scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. 10X PBMC 8k and Pan T cells) need a common low-dimensional embedding before batch-correction or integration comparison, and cells must be represented as points in N-dimensional space for distance-weighted matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction--23d39bdd60bafc66))
- **single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a single-cell expression matrix over unannotated transcriptionally active regions (uTARs) and need to determine which regions carry cell-type-defining signal via PC loadings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction--420b3de19d1f81e1))
- **single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a scRNA-seq pipeline needs PCA on highly variable genes with a data-driven, permutation-based choice of PC count, after read.data quality filtering and get.variable.genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction--4b50ec905faf1b99))
- **single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a normalized/scaled count matrix of barcoded single cells must be reduced to PC space before building a transcriptome nearest-neighbor graph for clustering or visualization (e.g., prior to ClonoCluster hybrid clustering or UMAP). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pca-dimensionality-reduction--2cabdcd9d37c7b7a))
- **single-cell-pca-latent-space-projection**: Use when a cell-by-gene or cell-by-peak AnnData matrix must be embedded before clustering or label transfer, e.g., scATAC-seq gene activity scores or a query dataset being projected onto a reference PCA via scanpy Ingest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pca-latent-space-projection))
- **single-cell-perturb-seq**: Analyze Perturb-seq and CROP-seq CRISPR screening data integrated with scRNA-seq. Use when identifying gene function through pooled genetic perturbations in single cells. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/perturb-seq) · ★1)
- **single-cell-perturbation-benchmark-analysis**: Use when comparing drug-responsive cell-type prioritization methods on scRNA-seq perturbation data where ground-truth target cell types are known and paired pre/post-treatment baselines must be evaluated against untreated-only inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-perturbation-benchmark-analysis))
- **single-cell-perturbation-differential-expression-annotation**: Use when a Perturb-Seq AnnData with raw counts and per-cell guide annotations must be screened for causal gene-gene interactions with calibrated FDR control, and VI-VS must be benchmarked against OLS and marginal tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-perturbation-differential-expression-annotation))
- **single-cell-perturbation-differential-testing**: Use when comparing perturbed vs control single-cell RNA-seq cells and asking which pre-annotated gene modules/pathways (GMT-defined GMVs) are significantly differentially active, with module-level significance and a GSEA cross-check. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-perturbation-differential-testing))
- **single-cell-perturbation-prediction**: Use when a multi-batch multi-condition scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., 3-batch COVID-19 atlas with age and disease severity condition types) requires predicting counts toward a held-out condition/batch combination, in-sample or out-of-sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-perturbation-prediction))
- **single-cell-perturbation-response-decoupling**: Use when analyzing scCRISPR-seq data (cell-by-gene matrices Y = perturbation group, Z = control group) where differing infected-cell cluster proportions between perturbation and control groups make fold-change perturbation effects unreliable and per-gRNA cellular response must be estimated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-perturbation-response-decoupling))
- **single-cell-plasticity-quantification**: Use when you have a reconstructed single-cell phylogeny (cells as leaves) plus per-cell categorical metadata (e.g., clone, site, or state labels from GEO metadata) and want a per-cell numeric score of how plastic (mixed) the ancestral context of each category is along the lineage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-plasticity-quantification))
- **single-cell-pluripotency-quantification**: Use when you need per-cell stemness/pluripotency scores from a normalized scRNA-seq count matrix (genes x cells) to identify stem/progenitor cells as trajectory origins, or to compare ORIGINS, LandSCENT, and CytoTRACE scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pluripotency-quantification))
- **single-cell-polya-expression-track-rendering**: Use when you need a per-gene genome-browser view (e.g., Fig. 1C) showing where poly(A) sites sit in a gene's 3'UTR and how single-cell pA counts and bulk BAM coverage change across sample groups such as SC/RS/ES. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-polya-expression-track-rendering))
- **single-cell-population-frequency-quantification**: Use when you hold a scRNA-seq reference atlas with a cell-to-type identity file (e.g., the CCI distribution's data_ref/ cell-type and identity files for healthy BMMCs) and must report or verify the percentage composition of each annotated population. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-population-frequency-quantification))
- **single-cell-population-rejection-evaluation**: Use when you need to test whether a hierarchical single-cell classifier detects truly novel populations: hold out a known population (e.g. 'Group 3' with four AMB nodes at tree layers 2 and 3) and measure the fraction of its cells rejected as unseen under alternative node classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-population-rejection-evaluation))
- **single-cell-population-separation-assessment**: Use when a trained deep learning model (Keras model JSON + weight bin, e.g. tmpMod./tmpWeight.bin) has classified single-cell gene expression data (e.g. Zheng sorted, Pancreas, CellBench) and you need to visually assess whether learned representations separate cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-population-separation-assessment))
- **single-cell-preprocessing**: Quality control, filtering, and normalization for single-cell RNA-seq using Seurat (R) and Scanpy (Python). Use for calculating QC metrics, filtering cells and genes, normalizing counts, identifying highly variable genes, and scaling data. Use when filtering, normalizing, and selecting features in single-cell data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/single-cell/preprocessing) · ★1)
- **single-cell-preprocessing-and-louvain-clustering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (cells x genes) need preprocessing and initial Louvain clustering prior to rare cell-type detection, e.g., as stage 1 of the scCAD cluster-decomposition workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-preprocessing-and-louvain-clustering))
- **single-cell-preprocessing-qc**: Use when raw CITE-seq count matrices (transcriptome + surface-protein/ADT) from 10x Genomics or similar must be cleaned and prepared — e.g. pbmc_1k_protein_v3-style datasets — before pairwise-distance fusion and Louvain clustering in LinQ-View. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-preprocessing-qc))
- **single-cell-preprocessing-with-omicverse**: Walk through omicverse's single-cell preprocessing tutorials to QC PBMC3k data, normalise counts, detect HVGs, and run PCA/embedding pipelines on CPU, CPU–GPU mixed, or GPU stacks. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-preprocessing) · ★3.1k · 15 copies)
- **single-cell-probability-calibration**: Use when a trained SIMS model must emit per-cell classification confidences, or when novel/unseen cell types in the target AnnData should be flagged as 'unknown' instead of receiving a forced label. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-probability-calibration))
- **single-cell-projection-outlier-detection**: Use when query cells have been projected into a reference atlas embedding (e.g., via ProjectSVR's ProjectNewdata with a ref.umap reduction) and you must determine which mapped cells are well-placed versus low-quality outliers before downstream label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-projection-outlier-detection))
- **single-cell-proportion-null-simulation**: Use when benchmarking false-positive control of cell-type proportion analysis: null datasets with unchanged cluster proportions are simulated and accuracy (fraction of unchanged clusters correctly called non-significant) is scored per replicate or pseudo-replicate count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-proportion-null-simulation))
- **single-cell-protein-ambient-noise-correction**: Use when raw ADT/protein count matrices from CITE-seq, ASAP-seq, or TEA-seq experiments show antibody-specific background from unbound antibodies, and both a cell protein matrix and an empty-droplet protein matrix are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-protein-ambient-noise-correction))
- **single-cell-protein-feature-matrix-analysis**: Use when a co-registered protein (IMC) and metabolite (TOF-SIMS) single-cell feature table is available and the goal is cell-population identification plus trajectory/pseudotime analysis of differentiation paths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-protein-feature-matrix-analysis))
- **single-cell-protein-intensity-normalization**: Use when you have a per-cell mean marker-intensity feature matrix from IMC single-cell segmentation spanning multiple imaging regions or patients, and you need comparable, normalized features for unsupervised cell-type clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-protein-intensity-normalization))
- **single-cell-proteomic-imaging-data-processing**: Use when processing preprocessed single-cell proteomic imaging (4i) .h5ad datasets (e.g., PDAC cell lines or RPE with 241–253 imaging features) to select features that preserve cell cycle phases or cellular trajectories prior to trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-proteomic-imaging-data-processing))
- **single-cell-proteomics-clustering**: Use when you have a per-cell mean marker-intensity feature matrix from single-cell segmentation of multiplexed IMC protein images (multiple imaging regions or patients) and need to label every segmented cell with a cell-type cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-proteomics-clustering))
- **single-cell-proteomics-data-interpretation**: Use when processed single-cell proteomics results (e.g., a ProteomeXchange dataset such as PXD037527) must be turned into reproducible figure panels that reproduce or match panels from a published study. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-proteomics-data-interpretation))
- **single-cell-proteomics-data-processing**: Load, inspect, centroid, and extract features from raw LC-MS/MS data files. This is **Step 1** of the proteomics pipeline — all downstream peptide identification and quantification steps require centroided, quality-checked spectra as input. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-proteomics-data-processing) · ★1.1k)
- **single-cell-proteomics-peptide-identification**: Search MS2 spectra against a protein sequence database to identify peptides and proteins in your sample. Apply target-decoy FDR filtering to control false discovery rate at both PSM and protein levels. ([PharMolix/OpenBioMed](https://github.com/PharMolix/OpenBioMed/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-proteomics-peptide-identification) · ★1.1k)
- **single-cell-proteomics-sample-metadata-mapping**: Use when you have per-run feature lists (m/z, charge, RT, intensity) from single-cell DIA-MS runs and need to associate each feature file with its cell/sample identity before cohort-wide RT alignment and alignment-vs-identification benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-proteomics-sample-metadata-mapping))
- **single-cell-pseudo-space-reconstruction**: Use when you have paired scRNA-seq and ST data (Visium, STARmap, Slide-seq) and need to map single cells into spatial coordinates or identify spatially variated cell subpopulations that traditional single-cell pipelines miss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudo-space-reconstruction))
- **single-cell-pseudo-temporal-ordering**: Use when a cell-to-landmark trajectory graph already exists and individual cells must be sequenced along developmental paths between adjacent landmark states to recover a pseudo-time axis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudo-temporal-ordering))
- **single-cell-pseudo-time-inference**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq count matrix captures cells from ordered developmental stages and the goal is to infer cell ordering (pseudo-time) and quantify it against known capture/stage time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudo-time-inference))
- **single-cell-pseudobulk-aggregation**: Use when a preprocessed scRNA-seq expression matrix (e.g. H5ad/HDF5/loom) must be converted into pseudo-bulk representative cells per cell type/sub-cluster before gene co-expression network inference with SCANet/WGCNA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudobulk-aggregation))
- **single-cell-pseudobulk-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether different scRNA-seq DS methods (edgeR/DESeq2/limma-voom pseudobulk vs MAST/dream mixed models) agree on top-ranked detections at FDR &lt; 0.05, or when benchmarking muscat's pbDS/mmDS engines on simulated multi-sample data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudobulk-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-pseudobulk-count-aggregation**: Use when you have raw scRNAseq count matrices with circadian (or other periodic) time labels and need bulk-like profiles so a predictor trained on bulk RNAseq or microarray data can score the samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudobulk-count-aggregation))
- **single-cell-pseudobulk-missingness-masking**: Use when individual-cell type pairs in scRNA-seq pseudobulk data have &lt;=10 cells so ctp/ctnu entries were set to missing, and CTMM fitting requires a complete expression and noise-variance matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudobulk-missingness-masking))
- **single-cell-pseudobulk-value-projection**: Use when pseudotime and branch probabilities were computed at the pseudobulk (neighbourhood) level via vdjPseudobulk and must be mapped back to individual cells in a miloR SingleCellExperiment, especially when some cells are unassigned to any neighbourhood. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudobulk-value-projection))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-assignment**: Use when snapshot scRNA-seq data (e.g. h5ad count matrices) lacks direction information and you need high-resolution, multi-branch trajectories plus per-cell pseudotime, especially with intricate topologies like parallel differentiation routes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-assignment))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-estimation**: Use when a processed AnnData with spliced/unspliced layers and scvelo RNA velocity plus Cytopath consensus trajectory steps are available and per-cell pseudotime and cell fate probabilities must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-estimation))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-inference**: Use when a single-cell gene expression matrix (cells × genes) must be ordered along a developmental trajectory, visualized in 2D, or split into branching differentiation paths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-inference--c17dc12d45495c3d))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-inference**: Use when a scRNA-seq expression matrix from a cross-sectional cohort must be ordered along a one-dimensional biological progression without capture times, especially when covariates (stimulus, mutation, drug) may perturb the trajectory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-inference--4a4542f6fff0543c))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-ordering**: Use when single cells have a sparse binary/eeSNV feature matrix with subgroup labels and the goal is a continuous genotype-based ordering (primary-to-metastasis trajectory) plus detection of subgroup-specific branches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-ordering))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when you have a single-cell scRNA-seq count matrix with a differentiation hierarchy (e.g., basal-to-differentiated epithelium) and need to root a trajectory objectively by pluripotency scores rather than a priori marker assumptions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-trajectory-inference--6dc51cdb19fbdc55))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-trajectory-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq data from multiple processing batches (e.g. the 10,878-cell human monocyte dataset) must be ordered along pseudotime without batch effects confounding the trajectory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-trajectory-inference--3ba363e102ac31b3))
- **single-cell-pseudotime-visualization-on-umap**: Use when a TFvelo velocity result (e.g. rc.h5ad from a completed run on pancreas or another dataset) needs pseudotime ordering, streamplot arrows on UMAP, and phase-portrait fitting plots for validation and manuscript figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-pseudotime-visualization-on-umap))
- **single-cell-publication-workflow**: Run, audit, annotate, statistically analyse and export publication-ready independent figures for human or mouse scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq projects using Seurat v5. Supports sample-level metadata manifests, configurable tissue-specific annotation dictionaries, and gated CNV, CellChat, Slingshot/tradeSeq, recurrent NMF programs, hdWGCNA, spatial transcriptomics, exploratory drug-response analysis and sample-aware virtual knockout with scTenifoldKnk. Never treat cells as biological replicates, never force unsupported annotations or advanced analyses, and never generate composite figures. ([Potato-AI0815/singlecell-publication-workflow](https://github.com/Potato-AI0815/singlecell-publication-workflow/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **single-cell-qbed-data-processing**: Use when the input is raw single-cell calling cards qbed/ccf insertion data and the goal is cleaned insertions, called binding peaks, genomic annotation, and a cell-by-peak AnnData/Mudata object ready for differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-qbed-data-processing))
- **single-cell-qc-cell-filtering**: Use when running gExcite (or its embedded scAmpi module) on hashed 10x PBMC samples where cell-level QC from the gene-expression layer must gate downstream CITE-seq/ADT analysis and antibody-positive cell calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-qc-cell-filtering))
- **single-cell-qc-filtering**: Use when raw Cell Ranger gene-expression matrices (e.g., the eFL_1 fetal liver sample) must be pre-processed into a high-quality cell population before clustering, using UMI/gene-count/mito-fraction thresholds and scrublet doublet removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-qc-filtering))
- **single-cell-qc-metrics-computation**: Use when raw droplet scRNA-seq FASTQ data (e.g. 10x GemCode, SRA SRP073767) must be turned into per-cell QC statistics such as median genes/UMIs per cell, capture efficiency, and multiplet rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-qc-metrics-computation))
- **single-cell-qc-review**: Review single-cell RNA-seq quality control from pasted metrics, plots or code, including MAD cutoffs and mitochondrial percent. Use for judging cell filtering, not clustering or model choice. ([Kukutx/superskills](https://github.com/Kukutx/superskills/tree/HEAD/plugins/science/skills/single-cell-qc-review))
- **single-cell-qc-summary-reporting**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (spliced and unspliced from scKB/kallisto/bustools) have been quality-filtered with COPILOT and you need to report or verify cell-filtering, mitochondrial thresholds, and doublet removal statistics in a CellRanger-3.1-comparable summary. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-qc-summary-reporting))
- **single-cell-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices (50kbp/5kbp bins or TSS regions) from scChIP-seq/scCUT&Tag/scChIC-seq must be cleaned of low-coverage and outlier cells before dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-quality-control-filtering))
- **single-cell-quality-control-filtering**: Use when single-cell readouts derived from SpaceM ablation-mark aggregation (h5ad/anndata objects) need QC prior to correlation, isotopologue, or uptake-fraction analysis, to remove poorly segmented or low-signal cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-quality-control-filtering))
- **single-cell-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw SPLiT-seq paired-end FASTQ reads need barcode/UMI identification and quality filtering, and more retained, mappable reads are needed than the original filter that discards reads whose last 6 bases fail to match the barcode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-quality-control-filtering--7a444242e1cf0d3e))
- **single-cell-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. GEO datasets like GSE145926, GSE132509, GSE123926/GSE113197) must be QC-filtered and normalized prior to Seurat integration, clustering, and downstream ASGARD drug scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-quality-control-filtering--e22005f9cea2848c))
- **single-cell-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices from scKB (spliced/unspliced 10x-format matrices) need background-noise/dying-cell removal before downstream Seurat analysis, RNA velocity, or trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-quality-control-filtering--72ae2557fba8701a))
- **single-cell-quality-control-preprocessing**: Use when raw single-cell counts (10x/h5ad) of representative samples must be quality-filtered and normalized, or when bulk cohort data must be preprocessed (normalize_total, log1p, top-HVG + markers, PCA) before scSemiProfiler VAE-GAN training or active-learning clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-quality-control-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-query-based-cell-type-annotation**: Use when annotating unlabeled scRNA-seq query cells against a curated reference with known cell types, especially across datasets with batch effects, and when negative queries (cell types absent from the reference) must be rejected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-query-based-cell-type-annotation))
- **single-cell-query-mapping-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a reference-mapping workflow (Symphony/Harmony) scales to large atlases and need wall-clock timings of reference building, query mapping, and de novo integration baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-query-mapping-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-rank-based-scoring**: Use when you need per-cell enrichment scores for gene signatures (e.g., cell-type or T-cell/myeloid marker sets) on a gene-by-cell scRNA-seq matrix, SingleCellExperiment, or Seurat object, and want scores invariant to dataset composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rank-based-scoring))
- **single-cell-rare-cell-labeling**: Use when you already have a per-cell FiRE rareness score vector and need a binary rare-cell label (0/1) per cell via the paper's IQR-based thresholding criterion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rare-cell-labeling))
- **single-cell-rare-cell-type-identification**: Use when scRNA-seq count or log-normalized expression matrices (cells x genes, e.g. .h5 with X/Y/gn/cn fields) need rare sub-populations below ~1% detected beyond global clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rare-cell-type-identification))
- **single-cell-rare-cluster-reclustering**: Use when a Seurat object contains a broadly-defined cluster suspected to be heterogeneous (low median silhouette scores) and rare subpopulations of very low abundance may be masked by standard unsupervised clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rare-cluster-reclustering))
- **single-cell-read-alignment-without-annotation-indexing**: Use when starting from raw scRNA-seq FASTQs and the goal is to quantify reads mapping outside gene annotations (uTARs) to assess annotation incompleteness, e.g. across human, mouse, lemur, mole rat, or sea urchin datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-alignment-without-annotation-indexing))
- **single-cell-read-barcode-assignment**: Use when demultiplexing noisy long-read single-cell RNA-seq data (e.g. a 61M-read PromethION FASTQ) against a barcode list of true and decoy barcodes, and you need read-barcode assignments plus computational cost comparison across tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-barcode-assignment))
- **single-cell-read-count-simulation**: Use when a benchmark needs simulated scRNA-seq read counts with ground-truth logFC or a co-expression-free null matrix — e.g. quantifying normalization/DE bias across expression bins or testing nonlinear confounder detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-count-simulation))
- **single-cell-read-deduplication**: Use when demultiplexing HIT-scISOseq or 10x Genomics-based long-read scRNA-seq FLNC reads requires correcting sequencing-error-derived cellBCs and merging UMI duplicates per gene/cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-read-deduplication))
- **single-cell-read-downsampling**: Use when planning or budgeting single-cell mtDNA clonality experiments: the input is an analysis-ready cell-barcoded BAM and the goal is to determine the minimum reads-per-cell depth at which variant calling (MQuad) and clonal assignment (vireoSNP) remain adequate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-downsampling--ce41a5d488afcbc3))
- **single-cell-read-downsampling**: Use when evaluating how an estimator of cell-level quantities (e.g., allelic proportions) performs under reduced per-cell sequencing depth, using full-depth single-cell read counts to create matched low-coverage replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-downsampling--b5c4be789c7e3acf))
- **single-cell-read-labeling-qc**: Use when raw paired-end SPLiT-seq FASTQ files need preprocessing to label reads with combinatorial barcodes and UMI, and identified-read counts/ratios must be reproduced or compared to published values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-labeling-qc))
- **single-cell-read-pseudoalignment**: Use when raw 10x scRNA-seq FASTQ files (e.g., SRR12046119/SRR12046120 Arabidopsis col0 with cDNA_introns_10xv3.idx, or human pbmc_1k_v2) must be pseudoaligned quickly and converted into raw spliced and unspliced gene-by-cell count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-pseudoalignment))
- **single-cell-read-splicing-status-prediction**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq reads aligned to a transcriptome need their spliced/unspliced status predicted, or when multi-gene mapped reads must be resolved to a single gene of origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-splicing-status-prediction))
- **single-cell-read-to-feature-assignment**: Use when quantifying TE expression or accessibility from single-cell RNA-seq or ATAC-seq alignments (unfiltered BAM/SAM with CB/CR and UB/UR barcode/UMI tags) and a cell-by-feature count matrix is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-read-to-feature-assignment))
- **single-cell-reclustering-low-purity-clusters**: Use when an scRNA-seq clustering partition contains clusters of questionable homogeneity and you need to quantify cluster purity with ROGUE and re-cluster low-purity clusters into purer subtypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reclustering-low-purity-clusters))
- **single-cell-reduced-dimension-analysis**: Use when you need to assess whether condition labels (e.g., treatment vs control) are locally imbalanced across a single-cell reduced-dimension representation before running trajectory-level differential topology or abundance tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reduced-dimension-analysis))
- **single-cell-reference-atlas-building**: Use when constructing a reusable integrated reference from large single-cell RNA-seq (or CITE-seq) expression matrices so that future query datasets can be mapped and annotated without re-running de novo integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-atlas-building))
- **single-cell-reference-atlas-model-construction**: Use when constructing a reference mapping model for a single-cell atlas (e.g. mTCA, DISCO PBMC, pan-cancer T cell landscape) by learning gene-set score → embedding relationships with ensemble SVR, to later project query scRNA-seq data without re-running integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-atlas-model-construction))
- **single-cell-reference-atlas-projection**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq clusters (e.g., a leukemia sub-cluster such as AML1_cluster1) must be assigned a healthy counterpart cell type from a reference atlas to infer lineage stage, leukemia subtype, or stemness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-atlas-projection--dd931f421ecc9444))
- **single-cell-reference-atlas-projection**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices (10x format or Seurat objects) must be interpreted in the stable coordinate system of a curated reference atlas, or when cell subtype composition of a query condition must be quantified and compared. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-atlas-projection--deb8865daaef6013))
- **single-cell-reference-based-purity-scoring**: Use when evaluating a segmentation method's cell-gene matrix for expression purity or cross-cell contamination, e.g. measuring B cells expressing T-cell markers (CD4, CD8A, CD8B, CD3D, CD3E) in Xenium breast cancer data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-based-purity-scoring))
- **single-cell-reference-based-segmentation-annotation**: Use when reproducing or extending the cross-platform generalisability benchmark of BIDCell on CosMx or MERSCOPE data, or when single-cell reference data are needed to guide biologically-informed segmentation and assess B-cell/T-cell marker contamination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-based-segmentation-annotation))
- **single-cell-reference-cell-type-annotation**: Use when segmenting subcellular spatial transcriptomics data (e.g. Xenium breast cancer) with BIDCell and a single-cell reference plus marker files are needed to pre-annotate cells for biologically-informed training or cross-replicate cell type proportion comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-cell-type-annotation))
- **single-cell-reference-deconvolution**: Use when bulk RNA-seq ExpressionSet data must be deconvolved into cell type proportions and a multi-subject scRNA-seq reference (ExpressionSet or SingleCellExperiment) with cell type and subject annotations is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-deconvolution))
- **single-cell-reference-mapping**: Use when a query scRNA-seq dataset (Seurat object, e.g. .qs/.rds) must be placed into a published reference atlas space for cell-type label transfer, cross-protocol batch-effect correction, pseudotime projection, or group composition comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-mapping--0c692b787842da28))
- **single-cell-reference-mapping**: Use when a query scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., a new 10x technology or donor) must be placed into an existing integrated reference embedding for label transfer, without re-running de novo integration on all raw cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-mapping--c8f800ff2c46a74e))
- **single-cell-reference-matrix-subsetting**: Use when building or reproducing a malignancy-classifier gene panel by intersecting pan-cancer bulk RNA-seq DE genes (e.g. TCGA edgeR top-5000 lists) with high-variance genes from an integrated scRNA-seq matrix, and subsetting that matrix into a classifier-ready reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-matrix-subsetting))
- **single-cell-reference-profile-generation**: Use when you have scRNA-seq or snRNA-seq counts with cell-type and individual labels from the same tissue as your bulk RNA-seq samples and need a reference profile for cell-type proportion estimation (decomposition). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-profile-generation))
- **single-cell-reference-proportion-estimation**: Use when you have a bulk or pseudobulk RNA-seq cohort (e.g., 124-sample COVID-19 pseudobulk) plus a single-cell reference (annotated representative samples or public dataset) and need cell-type proportions comparable against ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-proportion-estimation--d0623772fae4da57))
- **single-cell-reference-proportion-estimation**: Use when you have bulk RNA-seq read counts from complex tissue (e.g., 89 Fadista pancreatic islet donors) and a multi-subject scRNA-seq reference (e.g., E-MTAB-5060 Segerstolpe data), and need cell type proportions to test trait associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-proportion-estimation--5fc6b872bf956f26))
- **single-cell-reference-subsampling**: Use when evaluating whether a cell-querying/annotation method remains tractable as the reference dataset grows to hundreds of thousands of cells, by timing queries across a range of subsampled reference sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reference-subsampling))
- **single-cell-regulatory-network-inference**: Use when evaluating how well CSNI methods (e.g. pySCENIC, LIONESS, SSN) recover cell-specific regulatory interactions, and simulated single-cell data with known cellwise ground-truth GRNs is available for quantitative AUROC/AUPR evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-regulatory-network-inference))
- **single-cell-reprogramming-data-analysis**: Use when analyzing scRNA-seq time courses of TF-mediated reprogramming (e.g., 10x Chromium data with Monocle-annotated trajectory states) to identify and rank regulators of successful fate conversion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-reprogramming-data-analysis))
- **single-cell-research-planner**: Designs complete single-cell research plans from a user-provided biomedical direction. Always use this skill whenever a user wants to design, scope, or structure a single-cell study — including disease-focused, mechanism-focused, biomarker-focused, translational, perturbation-inspired, or validation-aware projects. It should define the research question, choose the best-fit study pattern, recommend sample grouping logic, suggest reference datasets as examples only, specify the core analysis modules, propose a validation ladder, and output four workload configurations (Lite / Standard / Advanc… ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome-med-research-skills/Protocol%20Design/single-cell-research-planner) · ★2.1k)
- **single-cell-rna-analysis**: Reproducible Scanpy workflow for human or mouse 10x scRNA-seq and snRNA-seq count matrices: single-sample descriptive QC, clustering and annotation, or comparative donor-aware pseudobulk DE and Milo DA; plus preflight validation, optional explicitly requested Harmony, checkpoints, resume, and a checksummed analysis bundle. Use for cell-called GEX matrices, not FASTQ, CITE-seq, ATAC, Multiome, spatial, trajectory, communication, or CNV analysis. ([PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S](https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-rna-analysis) · ★671)
- **single-cell-rna-analysis**: Perform a complete, lightweight single-cell RNA-seq analysis with Scanpy and Harmony: dataset selection, quality control, normalization, batch-aware embedding, clustering, cell-population annotation, marker genes, treatment-associated population shifts, pseudobulk comparisons, response signatures, visualizations, and review-ready artifacts. Use this as the default route for ordinary scRNA-seq analysis. Do not select scvi-tools unless the task explicitly needs a probabilistic latent model, semi-supervised label transfer, or Bayesian differential expression from verified raw integer UMI counts. ([Victor-Xu-1/X-Science](https://github.com/Victor-Xu-1/X-Science/tree/HEAD/skills/x-science/single-cell-rna-analysis) · ★41)
- **single-cell-rna-atac-integration**: Use when matched scRNA-seq and scATAC-seq data are available (e.g. 10X_PBMC_15K PBMC) and candidate distal regulatory elements must be linked to their putative target genes and validated against cis-eQTLs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-atac-integration))
- **single-cell-rna-count-denoising**: Use when raw UMI-based scRNA-seq counts (e.g., CITE-seq/REAP-seq RNA matrices, cTPnet_demo 4000-cell demo) must be denoised before protein imputation or other downstream modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-count-denoising))
- **single-cell-rna-count-normalization**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq UMI counts (e.g. 10x h5/h5ad matrices from GEO like GSE117825 or Synapse syn26320732) must be converted into log CPTT-normalized AnnData before scTriangulate integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-count-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-denoising-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how well a denoising method recovers true expression profiles from severely downsampled scRNA-seq data (70% transcript removal), particularly for rare cell states like pericytes, microfold cells, or microglial cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-denoising-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-rna-expression-classification**: Use when you have labeled cell-by-gene single-cell RNA-seq matrices (AnnData .h5ad or CSV) with a 'class_label' annotation and need automated label transfer to unlabeled cells, especially with many cell types or imbalanced labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-expression-classification))
- **single-cell-rna-label-transfer**: Use when you need to transfer cell-type annotations from a labeled single-cell RNA-seq reference (e.g., .h5ad AnnData with a 'class_label' column) to unlabeled query cells, even across datasets with different gene panels or cell lines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-label-transfer))
- **single-cell-rna-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when processing sci-L3 or similar single-cell RNA count matrices and per-cell depth metrics (e.g. cells above a 2000-read cutoff) to embed cells for clustering and cell-identity validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-pca-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-rna-qc**: Performs quality control on single-cell RNA-seq data (.h5ad or .h5 files) using scverse best practices with MAD-based filtering and comprehensive visualizations. Use when users request QC analysis, filtering low-quality cells, assessing data quality, or following scverse/scanpy best practices for single-cell analysis. ([anthropics/knowledge-work-plugins](https://github.com/anthropics/knowledge-work-plugins/tree/HEAD/bio-research/skills/single-cell-rna-qc) · ★37k · 2.5k installs · 82 copies)
- **single-cell-rna-qc**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/single-cell-rna-qc) · ★745)
- **single-cell-rna-qc**: Apply single-cell rna qc in reproducible local data workflows with version-aware APIs, explicit assumptions, and validation. Use when the user chooses single-cell rna qc or its strengths fit the task. ([sandbaseai/sandbase-skills](https://github.com/sandbaseai/sandbase-skills/tree/HEAD/marketing/single-cell-rna-qc) · ★212)
- **single-cell-rna-qc**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/External_Collections/life-sciences_Claudeai-main/single-cell-rna-qc) · ★36 · 6 copies)
- **single-cell-rna-qc**: scverse scRNA gold chain on .h5ad/.h5 — inspect, convert, MAD QC, optional scanpy.pp.scrublet, preprocess, Harmony/ComBat, PCA-UMAP-Leiden, Wilcoxon markers, subset, pseudobulk, pydeseq2, stable plots. Use when the user has scRNA-seq counts. Does not assign cell-type labels. rank_genes_groups is not condition DE. Do not call local doublet/ambient helpers SoupX, CellBender, or scDblFinder. ([HERRY423/BioNexus](https://github.com/HERRY423/BioNexus/tree/HEAD/plugins/bionexus/skills/single-cell-rna-qc) · ★32 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-rna-qc**: Performs quality control on single-cell RNA-seq data (.h5ad or .h5 files) using scverse best practices with MAD-based filtering and comprehensive visualizations. - **single-cell-rna-qc**: 对单细胞 RNA-seq 数据（.h5ad 或 .h5 文件）执行质控，使用 scverse 最佳实践的 MAD 过滤和综合可视化。适用于 QC 分析、低质量细胞过滤、数据质量评估或遵循 scverse/scanpy 单细胞分析最佳实践。 ([GACLove/feishu-aily-skills](https://github.com/GACLove/feishu-aily-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-rna-qc) · ★1)

Also annotates MT/ribo/hemoglobin gene sets, species-aware. Use when running QC from scratch on raw 10x or .h5ad data. - **single-cell-rna-qc**: Performs quality control on single-cell RNA-seq data (.h5ad or .h5 files) using scverse best practices with MAD-based filtering and comprehensive visualizations. - **single-cell-rna-qc**: Performs quality control on single-cell RNA-seq data (.h5ad or .h5 files) using scverse best practices with MAD-based filtering and comprehensive visualizations. Use when users request QC analysis, filtering low-quality cells, assessing data quality, or following scverse/scanpy best practices for single-cell analysis.
